# Public TypeScript API reference

Generated by `npm run docs:api`. Do not edit this file directly. Source JSDoc owns function behavior; the architecture guide documents transactions, authorization, errors, and planned APIs. Only declarations in current code appear here.

## [apps/desktop/src/electron/application.ts](../../apps/desktop/src/electron/application.ts)

Host storage for staying signed in. The remembered member must live in OS-protected storage;
the enabled flag defaults to on when nothing was saved.

```ts
export interface SessionMemory {
    enabled(): Promise<boolean>;
    setEnabled(value: boolean): Promise<void>;
    read(): Promise<RememberedMember | undefined>;
    write(value: RememberedMember | undefined): Promise<void>;
}
```

Desktop authority and operations. The opaque core context never leaves this host object.

```ts
class DesktopApplication implements ShopService {
  constructor(ports: WorkspacePorts, crypto?: ExchangeCrypto, files?: transfer.TransferPorts & {
    readonly onActivated: (database: Database) => Promise<void>;
    readonly onReset?: () => void;
}, memory?: SessionMemory, syncTransport: (url: string) => SyncTransport = sync.httpTransport);
  /** Completes durable secret/SQLite crash gaps before exposing the locked installation. */
  initialize(): Promise<void>;
  /** Automatic lock from the host. "away" (minimize, suspend, OS lock) is skipped while the user
   * chose to stay signed in; "integrity" (renderer loss/navigation) always locks but keeps the
   * remembered user for the next start. */
  autoLock(reason: "away" | "integrity"): Promise<void>;
  /** Invalidates authority immediately, then waits for native work before the caller closes SQLite. */
  dispose(): Promise<void>;
  /** Retries signing asynchronously; financial success never depends on signing or delivery. */
  seal(): Promise<void>;
  /** Invalidation-only subscription; observer errors cannot change command success. */
  onChanged(listener: () => void): () => void;
  /** Public boot metadata and current session; invalid/suspended contexts return locked views. */
  getWorkspace(): Promise<Result<WorkspaceView>>;
  /** Confirms the displayed locked/pending/legacy shop and switches to a migrated empty slot.
   * No credential proof is required to start a separate shop; old data/credentials are retained
   * and remain protected. Single-flight identity work and the slot CAS prevent overlapping swaps.
   * Failed staging/activation keeps the previous active slot; a successful switch returns activated. */
  startNewShop(input: unknown): Promise<Result<{ readonly activated: true; }>>;
  savedShops(): Promise<Result<readonly { readonly id: string; readonly name: string; readonly lastUsedAt: string; }[]>>;
  openSavedShop(input: unknown): Promise<Result<{ readonly activated: true; }>>;
  /** Strict fresh setup bound to host identity/platform, with protected native provisioning. */
  createShop(input: unknown): Promise<Result<WorkspaceView>>;
  /** Retries the existing durable setup intent with the supplied secret and no replacement identities. */
  finishSetup(input: unknown): Promise<Result<WorkspaceView>>;
  /** Single-flight local proof issues an opaque display session; lock cancels a pending proof. */
  unlock(input: unknown): Promise<Result<WorkspaceView>>;
  /** Immediately invalidates authority and in-flight proofs, and forgets a remembered user. */
  lock(): Promise<Result<WorkspaceView>>;
  sessionPreferences(input: unknown): Promise<Result<SessionPreferences>>;
  setRememberSession(input: unknown): Promise<Result<SessionPreferences>>;
  /** Erases this computer's shops after the typed shop name and the owner's code. The session is locked
   * and the remembered user forgotten before the host deletes files, after this command has answered. */
  resetComputer(input: unknown): Promise<Result<null>>;
  /** Owner recovery uses the separate protected key and leaves the installation locked. */
  recoverAccess(input: unknown): Promise<Result<WorkspaceView>>;
  /** Returns a protected owner recovery key only after current authority and fresh secret proof. */
  recoveryKey(input: unknown): Promise<Result<string>>;
  /** Bounded catalogue with optional stock according to current view capabilities. */
  listProducts(input: unknown): Promise<Result<Page<ProductView>>>;
  /** One scoped product under products.view, archived or not. */
  getProduct(input: unknown): Promise<Result<ProductView>>;
  /** Exact active/unambiguous barcode lookup; no prefix/name substitution. */
  scanProduct(input: unknown): Promise<Result<ProductView>>;
  /** A product's photo as a data URL under products.view; null when this computer holds none. */
  productPhoto(input: unknown): Promise<Result<PhotoView>>;
  /** Stores or replaces a product's photo under products.edit; the bytes are checked in core before anything is written. */
  setProductPhoto(input: unknown): Promise<Result<ProductView>>;
  /** Removes a product's photo under products.edit. */
  removeProductPhoto(input: unknown): Promise<Result<ProductView>>;
  /** Photo storage against the shop's limit. */
  photoUsage(input: unknown): Promise<Result<PhotoUsage>>;
  /** Creates product and optional opening stock in one transaction; safe matching retries reuse the product ID. */
  createProduct(input: unknown): Promise<Result<ProductView>>;
  /** Edits an active product's details from the displayed version. Unchanged requests (including
   * a retry after success) return the stored product; a newer stored version is never overwritten. */
  updateProduct(input: unknown): Promise<Result<ProductView>>;
  /** Archives or restores a product; repeating the same target state is a no-op. */
  setProductActive(input: unknown): Promise<Result<ProductView>>;
  /** Bounded categories under products.view, optionally with archived ones. */
  listCategories(input: unknown): Promise<Result<Page<CategoryView>>>;
  /** Creates a category named by its request ID; only this actor's identical creation counts as a retry. */
  createCategory(input: unknown): Promise<Result<CategoryView>>;
  /** Renames, archives or restores a category from its displayed version. */
  updateCategory(input: unknown): Promise<Result<CategoryView>>;
  /** Adds stock or records damage on an active product; the request ID is the movement ID. */
  moveStock(input: unknown): Promise<Result<ProductView>>;
  /** Records a shelf count; a retry returns the original difference instead of recounting. */
  countStock(input: unknown): Promise<Result<{ readonly deltaThousandths: number; readonly product: ProductView; }>>;
  /** Newest-first bounded stock movements of one product. */
  stockHistory(input: unknown): Promise<Result<Page<StockMovementView>>>;
  /** Bounded cash-account names only; balances are never exposed to payment-only readers. */
  listCashAccounts(input: unknown): Promise<Result<Page<{ readonly id: string; readonly name: string; }>>>;
  /** Account names and kinds for payments; balances stay behind finance authority. */
  listPaymentAccounts(input: unknown): Promise<Result<Page<PaymentAccountView>>>;
  /** Host-bound atomic sale with split payments and optional customer credit. `keptChangeMinor` puts the named
   * customer's change on their account (into the sale's first cash account, under customers.pay). Matching retries
   * (same actor, lines, payments, customer and kept change) return the original ID; differing content is refused.
   * The legacy `accountId` form records the exact total in one active cash account. */
  checkout(input: unknown): Promise<Result<{ readonly saleId: string; }>>;
  /** Bounded customers with balances under customers.view. */
  listCustomers(input: unknown): Promise<Result<Page<CustomerView>>>;
  /** One scoped customer under customers.view, archived or not. */
  getCustomer(input: unknown): Promise<Result<CustomerView>>;
  /** Creates a customer named by its request ID; only this actor's identical creation counts as a retry. */
  createCustomer(input: unknown): Promise<Result<CustomerView>>;
  /** Edits, archives or restores a customer from the displayed version; identical requests write nothing. */
  updateCustomer(input: unknown): Promise<Result<CustomerView>>;
  /** Takes a payment against an active customer's account; the request ID is the ledger entry ID. */
  receiveCustomerPayment(input: unknown): Promise<Result<CustomerView>>;
  /** Newest-first bounded customer ledger with signed amounts. */
  customerStatement(input: unknown): Promise<Result<Page<StatementEntryView>>>;
  /** Supplier and purchase commands; see purchasing.ts. */
  listSuppliers(input: unknown): Promise<Result<Page<SupplierView>>>;
  getSupplier(input: unknown): Promise<Result<SupplierView>>;
  createSupplier(input: unknown): Promise<Result<SupplierView>>;
  updateSupplier(input: unknown): Promise<Result<SupplierView>>;
  paySupplier(input: unknown): Promise<Result<SupplierView>>;
  supplierStatement(input: unknown): Promise<Result<Page<StatementEntryView>>>;
  recordPurchase(input: unknown): Promise<Result<{ readonly purchaseId: string; }>>;
  listPurchases(input: unknown): Promise<Result<Page<PurchaseSummary>>>;
  getPurchase(input: unknown): Promise<Result<PurchaseView>>;
  /** Account, cash and expense commands; see finance.ts. */
  listAccounts(input: unknown): Promise<Result<Page<AccountView>>>;
  createAccount(input: unknown): Promise<Result<AccountView>>;
  updateAccount(input: unknown): Promise<Result<AccountView>>;
  moveMoney(input: unknown): Promise<Result<AccountView>>;
  transferMoney(input: unknown): Promise<Result<AccountView>>;
  countCash(input: unknown): Promise<Result<CashCountView>>;
  correctCash(input: unknown): Promise<Result<CashCountView>>;
  listExpenses(input: unknown): Promise<Result<Page<ExpenseView>>>;
  recordExpense(input: unknown): Promise<Result<ExpenseView>>;
  reverseExpense(input: unknown): Promise<Result<ExpenseView>>;
  /** Period and snapshot reports; see reports.ts. */
  report(input: unknown): Promise<Result<ReportResult>>;
  /** Period analytics with permission-scoped sections; see reports.ts. */
  analytics(input: unknown): Promise<Result<AnalyticsResult>>;
  /** The operation journal under audit.view; see reports.ts. */
  journal(input: unknown): Promise<Result<AuditPage>>;
  /** Every journal entry matching the filters, for an export (audit.view and data.export); see reports.ts. */
  journalExport(input: unknown): Promise<Result<{ readonly items: readonly AuditEntry[]; readonly truncated: boolean; }>>;
  /** Owner only: facts held back for review; see reports.ts. */
  heldBack(input: unknown): Promise<Result<readonly HeldBackFact[]>>;
  /** Owner approves one held-back fact; see reports.ts. */
  reviewHeldBack(input: unknown): Promise<Result<{ readonly outcome: string; }>>;
  /** A spreadsheet export through the save dialog (data.export); see exports.ts. */
  saveCsv(input: unknown): Promise<Result<boolean>>;
  /** A report saved as PDF through the save dialog, under the report's permission; see exports.ts. */
  saveReportPdf(input: unknown): Promise<Result<boolean>>;
  /** Tax, payment terms and notifications; see settings.ts. */
  taxSettings(input: unknown): Promise<Result<TaxSettingsView>>;
  updateTaxSettings(input: unknown): Promise<Result<TaxSettingsView>>;
  productTax(input: unknown): Promise<Result<ProductTaxView>>;
  setProductTax(input: unknown): Promise<Result<ProductTaxView>>;
  paymentTerms(input: unknown): Promise<Result<PaymentTermsView>>;
  setShopTerms(input: unknown): Promise<Result<PaymentTermsView>>;
  creditStatus(input: unknown): Promise<Result<CreditStatusView>>;
  setPartyTerms(input: unknown): Promise<Result<CreditStatusView>>;
  notifications(input: unknown): Promise<Result<NotificationsView>>;
  markNotifications(input: unknown): Promise<Result<NotificationsView>>;
  /** Sale and purchase returns; see returns.ts. */
  returnSource(input: unknown): Promise<Result<ReturnSourceView>>;
  previewReturn(input: unknown): Promise<Result<ReturnPreview>>;
  recordReturn(input: unknown): Promise<Result<{ readonly returnId: string; }>>;
  /** Staff commands; see staff.ts. Credential work is single-flight with setup/unlock. */
  listStaff(input: unknown): Promise<Result<readonly StaffView[]>>;
  createStaff(input: unknown): Promise<Result<StaffView>>;
  setStaffPermission(input: unknown): Promise<Result<StaffView>>;
  setStaffRole(input: unknown): Promise<Result<StaffView>>;
  listRoles(input: unknown): Promise<Result<readonly RoleView[]>>;
  setRolePermission(input: unknown): Promise<Result<readonly RoleView[]>>;
  resetRole(input: unknown): Promise<Result<readonly RoleView[]>>;
  listCustomRoles(input: unknown): Promise<Result<readonly CustomRoleView[]>>;
  createCustomRole(input: unknown): Promise<Result<readonly CustomRoleView[]>>;
  renameCustomRole(input: unknown): Promise<Result<readonly CustomRoleView[]>>;
  setCustomRolePermission(input: unknown): Promise<Result<readonly CustomRoleView[]>>;
  deleteCustomRole(input: unknown): Promise<Result<readonly CustomRoleView[]>>;
  setStaffActive(input: unknown): Promise<Result<StaffView>>;

  /** The shop's pinned backup recovery keys (ADR-036), newest first; owner settings access. */

  prepareBackupKey(input: unknown): Promise<Result<PendingBackupKeyView>>;

  confirmBackupKey(input: unknown): Promise<Result<BackupKeyView>>;

  discardFile(): Promise<Result<null>>;
  saveBackup(input?: unknown): Promise<Result<SavedFile>>;
  chooseImportFile(input?: unknown): Promise<Result<{ readonly kind: "backup" | "invite"; }>>;
  openBackup(input?: unknown): Promise<Result<BackupFileView>>;
  restoreBackup(input?: unknown): Promise<Result<{ readonly activated: boolean; }>>;
  saveInvite(input?: unknown): Promise<Result<SavedFile & { readonly fingerprint: string; }>>;
  openPairingRequest(input?: unknown): Promise<Result<PairingRequestView>>;
  approvePairingRequest(input?: unknown): Promise<Result<SavedFile>>;
  openInvite(input?: unknown): Promise<Result<InviteView>>;
  acceptInvite(input?: unknown): Promise<Result<SavedFile & { readonly verificationCode: string; }>>;
  completePairing(input?: unknown): Promise<Result<{ readonly activated: boolean; }>>;
  cancelPairing(input?: unknown): Promise<Result<null>>;
  exchangeState(input?: unknown): Promise<Result<ExchangeView>>;
  saveExchange(input?: unknown): Promise<Result<SavedFile & { readonly empty: boolean; }>>;

  importExchange(input?: unknown): Promise<Result<{ readonly processed: number; }>>;
  saveImportReceipt(input?: unknown): Promise<Result<SavedFile>>;
  openImportReceipt(input?: unknown): Promise<Result<{ readonly recorded: number; }>>;
  retryExchange(input?: unknown): Promise<Result<{ readonly applied: number; }>>;
  /** Reads/resumes this installation’s enabled LAN listener and paired local delivery status. */
  localNetworkState(input: unknown): Promise<Result<LocalNetworkView>>;
  /** Stops inbound LAN access; local records and peer trust remain. */
  stopLocalNetwork(input: unknown): Promise<Result<null>>;
  /** Shows a ten-minute QR for a new device on the same Wi-Fi or hotspot. */
  startLocalJoin(input: unknown): Promise<Result<JoinCodeView>>;
  /** Reads the candidate name for the owner’s still-live local invitation. */
  pollLocalJoin(input: unknown): Promise<Result<JoinStatusView>>;
  /** Approves the local candidate and makes its encrypted snapshot available. */
  approveLocalJoin(input: unknown): Promise<Result<null>>;
  /** Revokes the selected local invitation. */
  cancelLocalJoin(input: unknown): Promise<Result<null>>;
  /** Immediately exchanges queued signed changes with this installation’s configured local hosts. */
  syncLocalNetwork(input: unknown): Promise<Result<{ readonly sent: number; readonly received: number; }>>;
  /** Empty computer: verifies a pasted join link or code before displaying the shop. */
  lookupLocalJoin(input: unknown): Promise<Result<JoinInvitation>>;
  /** Empty computer: provisions a native identity and asks the owner, through the LAN or the server mailbox. */
  requestLocalJoin(input: unknown): Promise<Result<null>>;
  /** Empty computer: activates the exact owner-approved shop when ready; it then connects to the owner's shared server on its own. */
  finishLocalJoin(input: unknown): Promise<Result<{ readonly activated: boolean; }>>;
  /** Optional sync server; see server-sync.ts. Network work runs in the main process only. */
  serverState(input: unknown): Promise<Result<{ readonly servers: readonly ServerView[]; readonly canEnroll: boolean; }>>;
  checkServer(input: unknown): Promise<Result<ServerPreview>>;
  connectServer(input: unknown): Promise<Result<ServerView>>;
  syncServer(input: unknown): Promise<Result<ServerSyncSummary>>;
  disconnectServer(input: unknown): Promise<Result<null>>;
  startJoinCode(input: unknown): Promise<Result<JoinCodeView>>;
  pollJoinCode(input: unknown): Promise<Result<JoinStatusView>>;
  approveJoinCode(input: unknown): Promise<Result<null>>;
  cancelJoinCode(input: unknown): Promise<Result<null>>;
  /** Rereads immutable saved receipt facts under current actor/own-receipt authority. */
  getReceipt(input: unknown): Promise<Result<Receipt>>;
  /** Bounded saved receipt history scoped to the current actor and sales-view capability. */
  listReceipts(input: unknown): Promise<Result<Page<ReceiptSummary>>>;
}
```

## [apps/desktop/src/electron/benchmark.ts](../../apps/desktop/src/electron/benchmark.ts)

```ts
benchmarkDesktop(window: BrowserWindow, database: Database, bootMs: number): Promise<void>;
```

## [apps/desktop/src/electron/database.ts](../../apps/desktop/src/electron/database.ts)

Open a caller-owned location and migrate it before exposing any business reads.

```ts
openDesktopDatabase(location: string): Database;
```

Read boot status; a missing shop requires setup rather than fabricated demo data.

```ts
desktopStatus(database: Database): DesktopStatus;
```

## [apps/desktop/src/electron/exports.ts](../../apps/desktop/src/electron/exports.ts)

Saves a spreadsheet the renderer assembled (the journal export) through the native save dialog, only for a member
holding `data.export`: writing shop data to a file of their choosing is the step that takes it off this computer.
The name must be a plain `.csv` name and the text is bounded like any file KOLO writes. False when cancelled.

```ts
saveCsv(host: TransferHost, input: unknown): Promise<boolean>;
```

Saves a report as an A4 PDF through the native save dialog, for a member holding the report's own permission and
`data.export` (a file of their choosing takes the figures off this computer, as for ). The figures are
read here exactly as  shows them; the renderer supplies only the wording (escaped into the page) and
the file name, a plain `.pdf` name. The shop name is the installation's. False when the dialog was cancelled.

```ts
saveReportPdf(host: TransferHost, input: unknown): Promise<boolean>;
```

## [apps/desktop/src/electron/finance.ts](../../apps/desktop/src/electron/finance.ts)

Accounts with derived balances under finance.accounts.view and cash.view.

```ts
listAccounts(host: Host, input: unknown): Page<AccountView>;
```

Creates an account named by its request ID; only this actor's identical creation counts as a retry.

```ts
createAccount(host: Host, input: unknown): AccountView;
```

Renames, re-kinds, archives or restores an account from the displayed version.

```ts
updateAccount(host: Host, input: unknown): AccountView;
```

Money in or out of one active account under cash.move; the request ID is the movement ID.

```ts
moveMoney(host: Host, input: unknown): AccountView;
```

Atomic transfer between two different active accounts; the request ID names the outgoing leg.

```ts
transferMoney(host: Host, input: unknown): AccountView;
```

Compares a count with the expected balance under cash.count; audit only, no money moves.

```ts
countCash(host: Host, input: unknown): CashCountView;
```

Corrects the books to a count; a retried correction returns its original result instead of correcting again.

```ts
correctCash(host: Host, input: unknown): CashCountView;
```

Newest-first bounded expenses under expenses.view.

```ts
listExpenses(host: Host, input: unknown): Page<ExpenseView>;
```

Records an expense from one active account; the request ID is the expense ID.

```ts
recordExpense(host: Host, input: unknown): ExpenseView;
```

Compensates one unreversed expense; the request ID is the reversal's ID.

```ts
reverseExpense(host: Host, input: unknown): ExpenseView;
```

A host-owned dispatcher map; none of its channel names are supplied by the renderer API.

```ts
desktopHandlers(current: DesktopApplication | (() => DesktopApplication), status: () => DesktopStatus, trusted: (event: unknown) => boolean): Readonly<Record<string, (event: unknown, ...args: unknown[]) => unknown>>;
```

## [apps/desktop/src/electron/host.ts](../../apps/desktop/src/electron/host.ts)

A stable command failure the host returns as-is; native and storage errors never cross the bridge.

```ts
class CommandError extends Error {
  code: FailureCode;
  constructor(code: FailureCode);
}
```

Whether an error is a refused input rather than a storage failure.

```ts
isValidationError(error: unknown): boolean;
```

What feature modules need from the desktop host: ports and the current actor, never the session itself.

```ts
export interface Host {
    readonly ports: WorkspacePorts;
    actor(data: Record<string, unknown>, required?: readonly Permission[]): OperationContext;
}
```

Local edits of the displayed version win locally even when this clock is behind the stored edit.

```ts
nextVersion(host: Host, stored: string): string;
```

Order-insensitive comparison of retried facts.

```ts
sameSet(left: readonly Record<string, unknown>[], right: readonly Record<string, unknown>[]): boolean;
```

## [apps/desktop/src/electron/join.ts](../../apps/desktop/src/electron/join.ts)

A join this computer opened: the network session and the candidate stay in the main process.

```ts
export interface OpenJoin {
    readonly start: JoinStart;
    readonly url: string;
    candidate?: JoinCandidate | undefined;
}
```

What join commands need: sync access, the snapshot port for the shop file, and this window's open joins.

```ts
export interface JoinHost extends SyncHost {
    readonly files: SnapshotPort;
    readonly joins: Map<string, OpenJoin>;
}
```

Shows a code (and QR link) for one new till on the first connected server.

```ts
startJoinCode(host: JoinHost, input: unknown): Promise<JoinCodeView>;
```

Whether the new till has asked yet; its name is shown for the owner to allow.

```ts
pollJoinCode(host: JoinHost, input: unknown): Promise<JoinStatusView>;
```

The owner allows the named till: its shop file is built, encrypted to it and handed over through the server.

```ts
approveJoinCode(host: JoinHost, input: unknown): Promise<null>;
```

Refuses or abandons a join; the code stops working at once.

```ts
cancelJoinCode(host: JoinHost, input: unknown): Promise<null>;
```

## [apps/desktop/src/electron/local-credentials.ts](../../apps/desktop/src/electron/local-credentials.ts)

Actual Node/OpenSSL implementation of the same native credential policy.

```ts
const desktopCredentialCrypto: CredentialCrypto;
```

The narrow Electron safeStorage dependency, injected so Node tests need no Electron process.

```ts
export type NativeSafeStorage = Pick<SafeStorage, "isAsyncEncryptionAvailable" | "getSelectedStorageBackend" | "encryptStringAsync" | "decryptStringAsync">;
```

OS-encrypted vault with atomic file replacement. Windows uses Electron/DPAPI;
unavailable encryption and Linux basic_text fail closed. Never writes plaintext
fallback or accepts a renderer path/key. Call after Electron readiness.

```ts
desktopSecretStore(directory: string, native: NativeSafeStorage, platform: string = process.platform): SecretStore;
```

Builds the desktop credential port using protected OS storage and async OpenSSL operations.

```ts
localCredentialsOnDesktop(directory: string, native: NativeSafeStorage): CredentialPort;
```

Native SHA-256 and Ed25519 verification, using the same canonical bytes as mobile/server.

```ts
const desktopExchangeCrypto: ExchangeCrypto;
```

Native authenticated file encryption; uses the measured credential KDF with independent per-file salt/nonce.

```ts
const desktopTransferCrypto: TransferCrypto;
```

Native X25519 and HKDF-SHA256 on top of the file-transfer AES-GCM, for end-to-end encrypted sync (ADR-030).

```ts
const desktopSealingCrypto: SealingCrypto;
```

## [apps/desktop/src/electron/local-network.ts](../../apps/desktop/src/electron/local-network.ts)

Owns one app-local HTTP listener. It transports authenticated pairing/encrypted facts; it has no separate database or account system.

```ts
class LocalNetwork {
  constructor(ports: WorkspacePorts, crypto: SealingCrypto, files: SnapshotPort, actor: () => OperationContext | undefined, changed: () => void);
  /** Current literal LAN addresses of this listener, preferring the common Wi-Fi range over container/VPN ranges. */
  urls(): string[];
  /** Whether this installation currently accepts direct local connections. */
  running(): boolean;
  /** Restarts the saved listener only while unlocked; first use chooses and persists an available port. */
  start(enable = true): Promise<void>;
  /** Stops accepting peers and forgets QR secrets immediately. Owned in-flight work is drained before database disposal. */
  close(disable = false): Promise<void>;
  /** Starts a single-use ten-minute local QR invitation. Only an address of this actual listener can be advertised. */
  invite(authorityAllowed: boolean, url?: string): Promise<JoinCodeView>;
  /** Reads a live invite's candidate name without granting device access. */
  status(id: string): JoinStatusView;
  /** Owner approval builds the encrypted baseline once and leaves it available until the QR expires. */
  approve(id: string): Promise<void>;
  /** Removes the mailbox and revokes an unapproved invite; already approved device trust is retained. */
  cancel(id: string): void;
}
```

## [apps/desktop/src/electron/pdf.ts](../../apps/desktop/src/electron/pdf.ts)

Renders a self-contained HTML page (a report from core `reportHtml`) to A4 PDF bytes in a hidden window that runs no
script, has no preload and may load nothing but the page itself. The window is destroyed whatever happens.

```ts
renderPdf(html: string): Promise<Uint8Array<ArrayBufferLike>>;
```

## [apps/desktop/src/electron/renderer-origin.ts](../../apps/desktop/src/electron/renderer-origin.ts)

The renderer is served from its own privileged scheme rather than `file://`: on a `file://` page a CSP `'self'`
also matches every other local file, while here it matches only the packaged renderer directory.

```ts
const RENDERER_SCHEME: "kolo-app";
```

The one page the window loads and the only frame trusted to call the command bridge.

```ts
const RENDERER_URL: "kolo-app://renderer/index.html";
```

What the renderer build contains; anything else is refused rather than guessed.

```ts
const RENDERER_TYPES: Readonly<Record<string, string>>;
```

Whether a frame URL is exactly the renderer page: same scheme and host, `/index.html`, no query or fragment.

```ts
isRendererUrl(candidate: string | undefined): boolean;
```

The renderer file a request names, or undefined: wrong scheme or host, a query, an encoded separator, a path that
leaves the renderer directory, or a file type the build never contains.

```ts
rendererFile(requestUrl: string, root: string): { readonly path: string; readonly type: string; };
```

## [apps/desktop/src/electron/self-check.ts](../../apps/desktop/src/electron/self-check.ts)

The desktop runtime checks itself: every crypto primitive against known answers in Electron's BoringSSL, and its
storage path (node:sqlite, every migration, the snapshot copy in a worker thread loaded from inside the asar).
`--self-check` runs this in a packaged build; electron-runtime.test.ts runs it in the test suite. A packaged build
passes its resources directory: electron-updater must load from the asar and the build must carry its update feed.

```ts
desktopSelfCheck(resources?: string): Promise<PlatformCheck>;
```

## [apps/desktop/src/electron/transfer-files.ts](../../apps/desktop/src/electron/transfer-files.ts)

Reads a selected regular file's bytes through one descriptor, capped even if it grows after the initial stat.

```ts
readBoundedTransferFile(path: string, maximum: number = MAX_TRANSFER_FILE_BYTES): Uint8Array<ArrayBufferLike>;
```

Opens a selected regular file for reading in pieces (ADR-035): a backup is never read whole. The size is fixed when the
file is opened; reading past it is refused, so a file that grows afterwards cannot feed more than was checked.

```ts
openTransferFile(path: string, maximum: number = MAX_TRANSFER_FILE_BYTES): Promise<PickedFile>;
```

Main-process native picker. Renderer IPC must authenticate its caller before invoking this adapter (F5).

```ts
pickDesktopTransferFile(native: Pick<Dialog, "showOpenDialog">, maximum: number = MAX_TRANSFER_FILE_BYTES): Promise<PickedFile>;
```

Main-process save dialog; publishes only a complete new selected file, never overwrites an existing business database.

```ts
saveDesktopTransferFile(document: TransferDocument, native: Pick<Dialog, "showSaveDialog">, maximum: number = MAX_TRANSFER_FILE_BYTES): Promise<boolean>;
```

Spreadsheet exports KOLO writes: a plain name ending in .csv, at most 100 characters.

```ts
const EXPORT_NAME: RegExp;
```

Main-process save dialog for a spreadsheet export; like transfer files it publishes a complete new file and never overwrites one.

```ts
saveDesktopExportFile(filename: string, bytes: Uint8Array, native: Pick<Dialog, "showSaveDialog">, maximum: number = MAX_TRANSFER_FILE_BYTES): Promise<boolean>;
```

Report PDFs KOLO writes: a plain name ending in .pdf, at most 100 characters.

```ts
const PDF_NAME: RegExp;
```

Main-process save dialog for a rendered PDF; like every export it publishes a complete new file and never overwrites one.

```ts
saveDesktopPdfFile(filename: string, bytes: Uint8Array, native: Pick<Dialog, "showSaveDialog">, maximum: number = MAX_TRANSFER_FILE_BYTES): Promise<boolean>;
```

Removes temporary files an interrupted streamed save left in the private outgoing directory.

```ts
clearOutgoing(directory: string): void;
```

Streams a large file (a backup) into a new private temporary file under `directory`, synced to disk, then offers it in
the save dialog and copies it chunk by chunk beside the chosen destination, publishing it whole only once synced and
never overwriting one. Temporary files are removed whatever happens, so an interrupted or failed save never leaves a
half-written file under a finished backup's name where the owner keeps backups.

```ts
saveDesktopTransferStream(write: (sink: ByteSink) => Promise<string>, native: Pick<Dialog, "showSaveDialog">, directory: string, maximum: number = MAX_TRANSFER_FILE_BYTES): Promise<boolean>;
```

## [apps/desktop/src/electron/transfer.ts](../../apps/desktop/src/electron/transfer.ts)

A file the owner picked, read by the main process in pieces; closing it releases the file.

```ts
export interface PickedFile extends ByteReader {
    close(): Promise<void>;
}
```

A picked file already in memory (tests, smoke runs).

```ts
pickedBytes(bytes: Uint8Array): PickedFile;
```

Native file access stays in the main process; the renderer never supplies paths or contents.

```ts
export interface TransferDialogs {
    open(): Promise<PickedFile | undefined>;

    saveExport?(filename: string, bytes: Uint8Array): Promise<boolean>;

}
```

What the desktop host provides for backup, pairing and exchange.

```ts
export interface TransferPorts {
    readonly files: SnapshotPort & {
        activeId?(): string;
        saved?(): Promise<readonly {
            id: string;
            name: string;
            lastUsedAt: string;
        }[]>;
        selectSaved?(id: string, target: Database): Promise<Database>;
    };
    readonly dialogs: TransferDialogs;
    readonly crypto: TransferCrypto;
}
```

What an imported file turned out to be.

```ts
export type ImportKind = "backup" | "invite";
```

One checked file per installation; checking another or locking forgets it.

```ts
export type StagedFile = {
    readonly kind: "backup";
    readonly preview: BackupPreview;
} | {
    readonly kind: "invite";
    readonly file: Uint8Array;
} | {
    readonly kind: "picked";
    readonly fileKind: ImportKind;
    readonly file: PickedFile;
} | {
    readonly kind: "request";
    readonly file: Uint8Array;
} | {
    readonly kind: "bundle";
    readonly preview: BundlePreview;
};
```

Host access for transfer commands, including the swap to an activated database.

```ts
export interface TransferHost extends Host {
    readonly transfer: TransferPorts;
    optionalActor(data: Record<string, unknown>): OperationContext | undefined;
    staged(): StagedFile | undefined;
    stage(file: StagedFile | undefined): Promise<void>;
    activate(database: Database): Promise<void>;
}
```

Core transfer and exchange failures become stable bridge codes.

```ts
translated<T>(work: () => Promise<T>): Promise<T>;
```

Import screen: one file dialog for a backup or an invite; the caller continues with the matching steps.

```ts
chooseImportFile(host: TransferHost, input: unknown): Promise<{ readonly kind: ImportKind; }>;
```

Streams the backup into a private temporary file, then offers it in the save dialog (ADR-035): memory stays a few MiB.

```ts

```

Opens the chosen backup with its password or with the shop's backup recovery key, exactly one of them (ADR-036).

```ts
openBackup(host: TransferHost, input: unknown): Promise<BackupFileView>;
```

Restores the checked backup. Signed in, the owner replaces the open shop. Signed out, an empty
computer restores directly; a computer with a shop needs `replace: true`, and its shop moves aside
as a safety copy (as starting a new shop always did) before the backup is restored into a fresh
slot. If that restore fails, the previous shop is selected again.

```ts
restoreBackup(host: TransferHost, input: unknown): Promise<{ readonly activated: boolean; }>;
```

```ts

```

```ts
approvePairingRequest(host: TransferHost, input: unknown): Promise<SavedFile>;
```


```ts
cancelPairing(host: TransferHost, input: unknown): Promise<null>;
```



```ts
saveImportReceipt(host: TransferHost, input: unknown): Promise<SavedFile>;
```

```ts
openImportReceipt(host: TransferHost, input: unknown): Promise<{ readonly recorded: number; }>;
```

```ts
retryExchange(host: TransferHost, input: unknown): Promise<{ readonly applied: number; }>;
```

Discards a staged backup snapshot and closes a picked file; other staged kinds hold only bytes.

## [apps/desktop/src/electron/updates.ts](../../apps/desktop/src/electron/updates.ts)

How this copy of KOLO was installed, which decides whether it can replace itself.

```ts
export type InstallKind = "nsis" | "appimage" | "deb" | "portable" | "store" | "development" | "other";
```

Reads the install kind from the runtime: electron-builder's portable launcher and AppImage runtime set these variables.

```ts
installKind(runtime: {
    readonly platform: string;
    readonly packaged: boolean;
    readonly windowsStore: boolean;
    readonly env: Readonly<Record<string, string | undefined>>;
    readonly packageType: string | undefined;
}): InstallKind;
```

The part of electron-updater's AppUpdater that KOLO drives; tests substitute a fake.

```ts
export interface Updater {
    autoDownload: boolean;
    autoInstallOnAppQuit: boolean;
    allowPrerelease: boolean;
    fullChangelog: boolean;
    checkForUpdates(): Promise<{
        readonly isUpdateAvailable: boolean;
        readonly updateInfo: {
            readonly version: string;
            readonly releaseNotes?: string | readonly {
                readonly version: string;
                readonly note: string | null;
            }[] | null;
        };
    } | null>;
    downloadUpdate(): Promise<unknown>;
    install(isSilent: boolean, isForceRunAfter: boolean): boolean;
    on(event: "download-progress", listener: (progress: {
        readonly percent: number;
    }) => void): unknown;
}
```

Whether going from `current` to `next` is non-breaking under semver's caret rule (`^current`): the same major from
1.0.0, the same minor before it, and never a pre-release.

```ts
nonBreaking(current: string, next: string): boolean;
```

A pre-release (`-beta.1`) is offered only to a copy that is itself on a pre-release.

```ts
offered(current: string, next: string): boolean;
```

The notes electron-updater read from GitHub, newest first and bounded; a missing note is left out.

```ts
releaseNotes(version: string, notes: string | readonly {
    readonly version: string;
    readonly note: string | null;
}[] | null | undefined): ReleaseNote[];
```

What the main process provides to .

```ts
export interface UpdateOptions {
    readonly kind: InstallKind;
    readonly currentVersion: string;
    readonly preferences: string;
    readonly updater: () => Promise<Updater>;
    readonly publish: (view: UpdateView) => void;
    readonly safetyCopy: () => Promise<void>;
    readonly beforeInstall: () => Promise<void>;
    readonly afterInstall: (started: boolean) => void;
    readonly now: () => string;
}
```

Checks GitHub Releases for a newer KOLO and, as the person chooses, downloads and installs it. Before 1.0.0 every
release is a GitHub pre-release, so pre-releases are read but a `-beta` version is offered only to a beta copy; from
1.0.0 only full releases are read. A found version is downloaded only on request, or in the background when automatic
updates are on and the version is non-breaking (). Every install first copies the shop database
(`safetyCopy`) and closes the shop (`beforeInstall`); the automatic install on close runs after the same copy and
KOLO's own shutdown, and is cancelled when the copy fails. The preference is a small file in user data, kept like the
other per-computer preferences when the computer is reset.

```ts
class DesktopUpdates {
  constructor(options: UpdateOptions);
  /** The state shown to the renderer. */
  view(): UpdateView;
  /** Checks shortly after start, then every six hours while KOLO stays open. */
  start(first = 20000, every = 6 * HOUR): void;
  /** Stops the scheduled checks. */
  dispose(): void;
  /** Looks for a newer version; a check already running, a download or a downloaded update is left as it is. */
  check(): Promise<UpdateView>;
  /** Downloads the version the last check found. */
  download(): Promise<UpdateView>;
  /** Copies the shop, closes it, starts the installer and quits. When the copy fails nothing is installed; when the
   * installer cannot start, KOLO restarts on its current version. */
  install(): Promise<UpdateView>;
  /** Before KOLO closes: copies the shop when an update will install on close, or cancels that install if the copy fails. */
  beforeQuit(): Promise<void>;
  /** Saves the preference; turning it on downloads a non-breaking update that was already found. */
  setAutomatic(enabled: boolean): UpdateView;
}
```

A consistent copy of the open shop database, taken before an update migrates it: `<version>-<time>.db` in `directory`
(private to this user), keeping the newest `keep`. It holds what the shop database holds and nothing more.

```ts
writeSafetyCopy(database: Database, directory: string, version: string, now: Date, keep = 3): string;
```

IPC handlers for the update bridge; like the shop handlers, untrusted senders and malformed arguments are refused.

```ts
updateHandlers(updates: DesktopUpdates, trusted: (event: unknown) => boolean, openDownloads: () => Promise<void>): Readonly<Record<string, (event: unknown, ...args: unknown[]) => unknown>>;
```

## [apps/desktop/src/electron/validation.ts](../../apps/desktop/src/electron/validation.ts)

Strict IPC object parser: rejects unknown fields, arrays and exotic prototypes.

```ts
object(value: unknown, keys: readonly string[]): Record<string, unknown>;
```

Bounded strings are checked before any database/native call; secrets retain intentional whitespace.

```ts
text(value: unknown, maximum = 120, trim = true): string;
```

Lowercase RFC UUIDs are portable record/request identities; native event IDs use the host UUIDv7 port.

```ts
uuid(value: unknown): string;
```

Numbers crossing IPC never coerce strings, fractional values, NaN or unsafe integers.

```ts
whole(value: unknown, minimum = 0): number;
```

Explicit boolean input, never truthiness.

```ts
boolean(value: unknown): boolean;
```

Absent optional fields stay absent; present ones must pass their own parser.

```ts
optional<T>(value: unknown, parse: (value: unknown) => T): T;
```

Bounded pagination/search, with no renderer-selected tenant or SQL.

```ts
page(input: Record<string, unknown>): PageQuery;
```

## [apps/desktop/src/electron/workflow-smoke.ts](../../apps/desktop/src/electron/workflow-smoke.ts)

Fixture dialogs: only the OS dialog is replaced. Saves and opens go through the same adapters as the app (a streamed
backup into a private temporary file, synced, then copied into a new file; files read in pieces), into the smoke
directory. "Open" returns the latest saved file, or the last backup from an earlier phase.

```ts
smokeDialogs(outgoing: string): TransferDialogs;
```

Disposable actual Electron DOM/IPC/native-vault workflow; invoked only by the explicit smoke flag.

```ts
runDesktopWorkflowSmoke(window: BrowserWindow, phase: string): Promise<void>;
```

## [apps/desktop/src/shared/service.ts](../../apps/desktop/src/shared/service.ts)

Stable application failures; native exception text, credentials and paths never cross the bridge.

```ts
export type FailureCode = "invalid_input" | "invalid_credential" | "retry_later" | "recovery_required" | "credential_unavailable" | "actor_inactive" | "permission_denied" | "scope_mismatch" | "setup_pending" | "locked" | "legacy_installation" | "already_configured" | "not_configured" | "busy" | "unavailable" | "request_conflict" | "price_changed" | "role_in_use" | "duplicate_name" | "storage_failed" | "credit_limit" | "stale_return" | "local_network_unavailable" | "invalid_file" | "too_large" | "invalid_password" | "invalid_recovery_key" | "recovery_key_required" | "untrusted_backup" | "authentication_failed" | "unsupported_version" | "invalid_snapshot" | "confirmation_required" | "not_empty" | "pairing_pending" | "pairing_expired" | "pairing_conflict" | "invalid_pairing" | "activation_failed" | "cancelled" | "no_file" | "sync_keys_unavailable" | "inconsistent_records" | "invalid_photo" | "photo_storage_full" | "server_unreachable" | "server_refused" | "fingerprint_mismatch" | "invalid_address" | "invalid_code" | "invalid_connection_code" | "connection_code_used" | "connection_code_expired" | "enrollment_conflict" | "server_not_approved" | "shop_suspended" | "shop_removed" | "hosted_service_inactive" | "no_server" | "join_not_found" | "join_conflict" | "not_join_code" | "invalid_join_code" | "join_address_needed";
```

Serializable results make transport and operation failures explicit.

```ts
export type Result<T> = {
    readonly ok: true;
    readonly value: T;
} | {
    readonly ok: false;
    readonly error: {
        readonly code: FailureCode;
        readonly retryAt?: string;
    };
};
```

Public installation metadata, with a transient session marker rather than an operation capability.

```ts
export interface WorkspaceView {
    readonly state: "empty" | "legacy" | "pending" | "locked" | "unlocked";
    readonly shopName: string | null;
    readonly members: readonly {
        readonly id: string;
        readonly name: string;
    }[];
    readonly actor: {
        readonly name: string;
        readonly isOwner: boolean;
        readonly permissions: readonly Permission[];
    } | null;
    readonly sessionId: string | null;
}
```

Bounded product facts safe for DOM clients; authority, provenance and secret references stay in the host.

```ts
export interface ProductView {
    readonly id: string;
    readonly name: string;
    readonly unit: string;
    readonly divisible: boolean;
    readonly barcode?: string;
    readonly sellingPriceMinor: number;
    readonly purchasePriceMinor?: number;
    readonly onHandThousandths?: number;
    readonly categoryId?: string;
    readonly minimumStockThousandths: number;
    readonly active: boolean;
    readonly version: string;
    readonly photo?: string;
    readonly otherCategoryIds?: readonly string[];
}
```

Catalogue grouping. Archived categories remain attached to existing products.

```ts
export interface CategoryView {
    readonly id: string;
    readonly name: string;
    readonly active: boolean;
    readonly version: string;
}
```

One immutable stock movement; corrections appear as later compensating rows.

```ts
export interface StockMovementView {
    readonly id: string;
    readonly occurredAt: string;
    readonly type: string;
    readonly quantityThousandths: number;
    readonly reason?: string;
}
```

Catalogue pages may include archived rows for restoring them.

`categoryId` (products only) keeps products whose main or additional category it is.

```ts
export type CatalogueQuery = SessionRequest & PageQuery & {
    readonly includeInactive?: boolean;
    readonly categoryId?: string;
};
```

Every protected call binds to the currently displayed actor session, including queued calls.

```ts
export interface SessionRequest {
    readonly sessionId: string;
}
```

Fresh shop setup. The host supplies identity, platform, currency and initial account policy.

```ts
export interface SetupRequest {
    readonly businessName: string;
    readonly ownerName: string;
    readonly deviceLabel: string;
    readonly cashAccountName: string;
    readonly secret: string;
}
```

Stable request identity doubles as the created product identity for safe creation retries.

```ts
export interface ProductRequest extends SessionRequest {
    readonly requestId: string;
    readonly name: string;
    readonly unit: string;
    readonly divisible: boolean;
    readonly barcode?: string;
    readonly sellingPriceMinor: number;
    readonly purchasePriceMinor?: number;
    readonly openingThousandths: number;
    readonly categoryId?: string;
}
```

Detail edit made from a displayed version. An identical retry returns the stored product;
any other change since that version is refused rather than overwritten. Whole/fractional
quantities and archive state are not edited here.

```ts
export interface ProductUpdateRequest extends SessionRequest {
    readonly productId: string;
    readonly version: string;
    readonly name: string;
    readonly unit: string;
    readonly barcode?: string;
    readonly sellingPriceMinor: number;
    readonly purchasePriceMinor?: number;
    readonly categoryId?: string;
    readonly minimumStockThousandths: number;
    readonly otherCategoryIds?: readonly string[];
}
```

Manual stock received outside a supplier purchase, or stock lost to damage. The request ID names the movement.

```ts
export interface StockMoveRequest extends SessionRequest {
    readonly requestId: string;
    readonly productId: string;
    readonly kind: "add" | "damage";
    readonly quantityThousandths: number;
    readonly reason: string;
}
```

Observed shelf quantity; the host records the difference from current stock at the moment it commits.

```ts
export interface StockCountRequest extends SessionRequest {
    readonly requestId: string;
    readonly productId: string;
    readonly countedThousandths: number;
    readonly reason: string;
}
```

Checkout carries only selected facts; the host derives actor, shop, time, stock and event IDs.
`payments` (one per account) may be split and may leave a balance on the named customer's
account. The legacy single `accountId` form records the exact total in that cash account.

```ts
export interface CheckoutRequest extends SessionRequest {
    readonly requestId: string;
    readonly accountId?: string;
    readonly payments?: readonly {
        readonly accountId: string;
        readonly amountMinor: number;
    }[];
    readonly customerId?: string;
    readonly expectedCustomerBalanceMinor?: number;
    readonly discountMinor?: number;
    readonly keptChangeMinor?: number;
    readonly lines: readonly {
        readonly productId: string;
        readonly quantity: number;
        readonly unitPriceMinor: number;
    }[];
}
```

Account names and kinds for taking money, never balances.

```ts
export interface PaymentAccountView {
    readonly id: string;
    readonly name: string;
    readonly kind: "CASH" | "MOBILE_MONEY" | "BANK" | "CARD" | "OTHER";
}
```

Customer details and current balance (positive: owed to the shop; negative: paid in advance).

```ts
export interface CustomerView {
    readonly id: string;
    readonly name: string;
    readonly phone?: string;
    readonly address?: string;
    readonly notes?: string;
    readonly creditLimitMinor?: number;
    readonly outstandingMinor: number;
    readonly active: boolean;
    readonly version: string;
}
```

Editable customer details; absent optional fields are cleared.

```ts
export interface CustomerDetails {
    readonly name: string;
    readonly phone?: string;
    readonly address?: string;
    readonly notes?: string;
    readonly creditLimitMinor?: number;
}
```

One immutable customer ledger entry, newest first; payments are negative.

```ts
export interface StatementEntryView {
    readonly id: string;
    readonly occurredAt: string;
    readonly type: string;
    readonly amountMinor: number;
    readonly saleId?: string;
    readonly purchaseId?: string;
    readonly note?: string;
    readonly saleTotalMinor?: number;
    readonly salePaidMinor?: number;
    readonly balanceMinor: number;
}
```

Supplier details and current payable (positive: the shop owes them).

```ts
export interface SupplierView {
    readonly id: string;
    readonly name: string;
    readonly phone?: string;
    readonly address?: string;
    readonly contact?: string;
    readonly notes?: string;
    readonly outstandingMinor: number;
    readonly active: boolean;
    readonly version: string;
}
```

Editable supplier details; absent optional fields are cleared.

```ts
export interface SupplierDetails {
    readonly name: string;
    readonly phone?: string;
    readonly address?: string;
    readonly contact?: string;
    readonly notes?: string;
}
```

A delivery received into stock at recorded unit costs. An optional single door payment is
taken from one account; any remainder is owed to the named supplier.

```ts
export interface PurchaseRequest extends SessionRequest {
    readonly requestId: string;
    readonly supplierId?: string;
    readonly reference?: string;
    readonly lines: readonly {
        readonly productId: string;
        readonly quantity: number;
        readonly unitCostMinor: number;
    }[];
    readonly payment?: {
        readonly accountId: string;
        readonly amountMinor: number;
    };
}
```

Purchase history row with exact total from historical costs.

```ts
export interface PurchaseSummary {
    readonly id: string;
    readonly occurredAt: string;
    readonly supplierName: string;
    readonly reference: string;
    readonly totalMinor: number;
}
```

Immutable recorded delivery.

```ts
export interface PurchaseView extends PurchaseSummary {
    readonly supplierId?: string;
    readonly lines: readonly {
        readonly id: string;
        readonly productId: string;
        readonly name: string;
        readonly quantity: number;
        readonly unitCostMinor: number;
    }[];
    readonly paidMinor: number;
    readonly outstandingMinor: number;
}
```

Account kinds: an account is where money is, never a payment method.

```ts
export type AccountKind = PaymentAccountView["kind"];
```

A financial account with its derived balance.

```ts
export interface AccountView {
    readonly id: string;
    readonly name: string;
    readonly kind: AccountKind;
    readonly active: boolean;
    readonly version: string;
    readonly onHandMinor?: number;
}
```

Expense categories shared with mobile and reports.

```ts
export type ExpenseCategory = "RENT" | "ELECTRICITY" | "WATER" | "TRANSPORT" | "SALARY" | "PACKAGING" | "MAINTENANCE" | "INTERNET" | "MARKETING" | "OTHER";
```

One immutable expense; reversals are later negative entries naming the original.

```ts
export interface ExpenseView {
    readonly id: string;
    readonly occurredAt: string;
    readonly category: ExpenseCategory;
    readonly amountMinor: number;
    readonly accountId: string;
    readonly accountName: string;
    readonly description?: string;
    readonly reference?: string;
    readonly reversesId?: string;
    readonly reversed: boolean;
}
```

Expected balance against a counted amount; a correction makes the books match the count.

```ts
export interface CashCountView {
    readonly expectedMinor: number;
    readonly countedMinor: number;
    readonly differenceMinor: number;
}
```

What remains returnable from a sale or purchase, at the original net prices or costs.

```ts
export interface ReturnSourceView {
    readonly kind: "SALE" | "PURCHASE";
    readonly originalId: string;
    readonly priorId?: string;
    readonly hasCounterparty: boolean;
    readonly refundableMinor: number;
    readonly items: readonly {
        readonly id: string;
        readonly name: string;
        readonly originalQuantity: number;
        readonly returnedQuantity: number;
    }[];
}
```

Exact value of a selected return before it is recorded.

```ts
export interface ReturnPreview {
    readonly totalMinor: number;
    readonly lines: readonly {
        readonly originalItemId: string;
        readonly quantity: number;
        readonly amountMinor: number;
    }[];
}
```

Records a partial or full return against the predecessor the preview was made from. The
refund (sale: paid out; purchase: received back) is at most the value and at most the source's
`refundableMinor`; any remainder goes to the customer's or supplier's ledger. A return recorded meanwhile makes this one stale.

```ts
export interface ReturnRequest extends SessionRequest {
    readonly requestId: string;
    readonly kind: "SALE" | "PURCHASE";
    readonly originalId: string;
    readonly priorId?: string;
    readonly reason: string;
    readonly items: readonly {
        readonly originalItemId: string;
        readonly quantity: number;
    }[];
    readonly refund?: {
        readonly accountId: string;
        readonly amountMinor: number;
    };
}
```

A local staff member with effective permissions; no credential material is exposed. `exceptions` are the
permissions where the member differs from their role: a grant the role lacks or a revocation of one it gives.

```ts
export interface StaffView {
    readonly id: string;
    readonly name: string;
    readonly role: "OWNER" | "MANAGER" | "CASHIER" | "INVENTORY" | "ACCOUNTANT";
    readonly active: boolean;
    readonly isSelf: boolean;
    readonly permissions: readonly Permission[];
    readonly exceptions: readonly Permission[];
    readonly customRole?: {
        readonly id: string;
        readonly name: string;
        readonly deleted: boolean;
    };
}
```

One column of the shop's role matrix (ADR-037); `customized` is false while the role keeps its built-in defaults.

```ts
export interface RoleView {
    readonly role: Exclude<StaffView["role"], "OWNER">;
    readonly permissions: readonly Permission[];
    readonly customized: boolean;
}
```

A shop-defined role: a named copy of a built-in staff role with its own permissions. A deleted role is listed only
while members still hold it, and can no longer be assigned.

```ts
export interface CustomRoleView {
    readonly id: string;
    readonly name: string;
    readonly baseRole: RoleView["role"];
    readonly permissions: readonly Permission[];
    readonly deleted: boolean;
    readonly holders: number;
}
```

A checked backup file waiting for an explicit restore confirmation.

```ts
export interface BackupFileView {
    readonly businessName: string;
    readonly ownerName: string;
    readonly createdAt: string;
    readonly appliedEvents: number;
}
```

A checked pairing invite: compare the fingerprint with the owner's screen before accepting.

```ts
export interface InviteView {
    readonly businessName: string;
    readonly ownerName: string;
    readonly fingerprint: string;
    readonly authorityAllowed: boolean;
}
```

A checked pairing request: compare the code with the new till's screen before approving.

```ts
export interface PairingRequestView {
    readonly deviceLabel: string;
    readonly businessName: string;
    readonly verificationCode: string;
    readonly authorityAllowed: boolean;
}
```

Paired destinations, received batches and actionable diagnostics.

```ts
export interface ExchangeView {
    readonly destinations: readonly {
        readonly id: string;
        readonly label: string;
        readonly pending: number;
    }[];
    readonly batches: readonly {
        readonly batchId: string;
        readonly senderDeviceId: string;
        readonly createdAt: string;
    }[];
    readonly diagnostics: readonly {
        readonly eventId: string;
        readonly code: string;
        readonly description: string;
        readonly action: string;
    }[];
}
```

A connected sync server as this computer sees it; `pending` counts facts not yet acknowledged by it. `automatic` is
true when this computer followed the owner's shared server address. `shareCode` (owner only) lets the shop's other
tills connect: it carries the address and fingerprint but no single-use token.

```ts
export interface ServerView {
    readonly destinationId: string;
    readonly url: string;
    readonly fingerprint: string;
    readonly connectedAt: string;
    readonly lastAttemptAt: string | null;
    readonly lastSuccessAt: string | null;
    readonly lastError: string | null;
    readonly pending: number;
    readonly automatic: boolean;
    readonly shareCode: string | null;
    readonly service: "unlimited" | "active" | "grace" | "inactive" | null;
    readonly paidUntil: string | null;
    readonly graceUntil: string | null;
    readonly maxTills: number | null;
    readonly tills: number | null;
}
```

The server a pasted connection code points to, checked against the fingerprint the code carries.

```ts
export interface ServerPreview {
    readonly url: string;
    readonly fingerprint: string;
    readonly canEnroll: boolean;
    readonly hasToken: boolean;
    readonly attached: boolean;
}
```

A code shown to add one till (ADR-032); `link` is what the QR code carries.

```ts
export interface JoinCodeView {
    readonly joinId: string;
    readonly code: string;
    readonly link: string;
    readonly url: string;
    readonly expiresAt: string;
    readonly authorityAllowed: boolean;
}
```

App-local listener addresses and this computer's paired local hosts. Internet and a separately installed server are unnecessary.

```ts
export interface LocalNetworkView {
    readonly running: boolean;
    readonly urls: readonly string[];
    readonly connections: readonly LocalPeerConnection[];
}
```

Whether the new till has asked to join; its name is shown before the owner allows it.

```ts
export type JoinStatusView = {
    readonly state: "waiting" | "expired";
} | {
    readonly state: "requested";
    readonly label: string;
    readonly platform: string;
};
```

One synchronization pass; `untrusted` facts wait for the owner's approval of their device, `locked` facts for a shop key.

```ts
export interface ServerSyncSummary {
    readonly pushed: number;
    readonly rejected: number;
    readonly pulled: number;
    readonly untrusted: number;
    readonly locked: number;
}
```

Assessment of a checked exchange file before import.

```ts
export interface BundleView {
    readonly total: number;
    readonly ready: number;
    readonly duplicate: number;
    readonly deferred: number;
    readonly review: number;
}
```

Whether a native save dialog wrote the prepared file (false when the user cancelled).

```ts
export interface SavedFile {
    readonly saved: boolean;
}
```

The shop's tax settings (ADR-033): prices include tax. `version` is absent until first saved.

```ts
export interface TaxSettingsView {
    readonly enabled: boolean;
    readonly label: string;
    readonly standardRateBasisPoints: number;
    readonly registration?: string;
    readonly version?: string;
}
```

A product's tax rule and the rate a sale of it carries now (0 while tax is off).

```ts
export interface ProductTaxView {
    readonly rule: "STANDARD" | "EXEMPT" | "RATE";
    readonly rateBasisPoints?: number;
    readonly effectiveRateBasisPoints: number;
    readonly version?: string;
}
```

The shop's default payment terms in days; 30 when never saved (`stored: false`).

```ts
export interface PaymentTermsView {
    readonly customer: {
        readonly days: number;
        readonly stored: boolean;
        readonly version?: string;
    };
    readonly supplier: {
        readonly days: number;
        readonly stored: boolean;
        readonly version?: string;
    };
}
```

A customer's or supplier's terms and standing now. Payments settle the oldest debt first; `overdueMinor` is unpaid
debt past its due date, `dueSoonMinor` falls due within three days. `version` names the party's own terms record.

```ts
export interface CreditStatusView {
    readonly days: number;
    readonly source: "party" | "shop" | "default";
    readonly partyDays?: number;
    readonly version?: string;
    readonly balanceMinor: number;
    readonly overdueMinor: number;
    readonly dueSoonMinor: number;
    readonly oldestDueAt?: string;
    readonly nextDueAt?: string;
}
```

Current notifications (derived locally) with the unread count.

```ts
export interface NotificationsView {
    readonly items: readonly ShopNotification[];
    readonly unread: number;
    readonly truncated: boolean;
}
```

Platform-neutral UI service. Electron implements it with named preload calls; a future web adapter can replace it.

A pinned backup recovery key: its ID, version, creation date and whether new backups seal to it.

```ts
export interface BackupKeyView {
    readonly keyId: string;
    readonly version: number;
    readonly createdAt: string;
    readonly current: boolean;
}
```

A backup recovery key to show once: its 14 groups, its ID and the 0-based groups the owner must type back.

```ts
export interface PendingBackupKeyView {
    readonly text: string;
    readonly keyId: string;
    readonly confirm: readonly number[];
}
```

Per-computer session behaviour.

```ts
export interface SessionPreferences {
    readonly rememberSession: boolean;
}
```

A product photo ready for an <img>: a data URL (the renderer CSP allows data: images only).

```ts
export interface PhotoView {
    readonly digest: string;
    readonly dataUrl: string;
}
```

Photos held by the shop on this computer against the active storage limit.

```ts
export interface PhotoUsage {
    readonly photos: number;
    readonly bytes: number;
    readonly limitBytes: number;
}
```

```ts
export interface ShopService {
    localNetworkState(input: SessionRequest): Promise<Result<LocalNetworkView>>;
    stopLocalNetwork(input: SessionRequest): Promise<Result<null>>;
    startLocalJoin(input: SessionRequest & {
        readonly authorityAllowed: boolean;
        readonly url?: string;
    }): Promise<Result<JoinCodeView>>;
    pollLocalJoin(input: SessionRequest & {
        readonly joinId: string;
    }): Promise<Result<JoinStatusView>>;
    approveLocalJoin(input: SessionRequest & {
        readonly joinId: string;
    }): Promise<Result<null>>;
    cancelLocalJoin(input: SessionRequest & {
        readonly joinId: string;
    }): Promise<Result<null>>;
    syncLocalNetwork(input: SessionRequest): Promise<Result<{
        readonly sent: number;
        readonly received: number;
    }>>;
    lookupLocalJoin(input: {
        readonly link: string;
        readonly url?: string;
    }): Promise<Result<JoinInvitation>>;
    requestLocalJoin(input: {
        readonly link: string;
        readonly url?: string;
        readonly deviceLabel: string;
        readonly secret: string;
    }): Promise<Result<null>>;
    finishLocalJoin(input: {
        readonly link: string;
        readonly url?: string;
    }): Promise<Result<{
        readonly activated: boolean;
    } | null>>;
    getWorkspace(): Promise<Result<WorkspaceView>>;
    startNewShop(input: {
        readonly confirmedName: string;
    }): Promise<Result<{
        readonly activated: true;
    }>>;
    savedShops(): Promise<Result<readonly {
        readonly id: string;
        readonly name: string;
        readonly lastUsedAt: string;
    }[]>>;
    openSavedShop(input: {
        readonly id: string;
    }): Promise<Result<{
        readonly activated: true;
    }>>;
    createShop(input: SetupRequest): Promise<Result<WorkspaceView>>;
    finishSetup(input: {
        readonly secret: string;
    }): Promise<Result<WorkspaceView>>;
    unlock(input: {
        readonly userId: string;
        readonly secret: string;
    }): Promise<Result<WorkspaceView>>;
    lock(): Promise<Result<WorkspaceView>>;
    recoverAccess(input: {
        readonly recoveryCode: string;
        readonly newSecret: string;
    }): Promise<Result<WorkspaceView>>;
    sessionPreferences(input: SessionRequest): Promise<Result<SessionPreferences>>;
    setRememberSession(input: SessionRequest & {
        readonly enabled: boolean;
    }): Promise<Result<SessionPreferences>>;
    resetComputer(input: SessionRequest & {
        readonly confirmedName: string;
        readonly secret: string;
    }): Promise<Result<null>>;
    recoveryKey(input: SessionRequest & {
        readonly secret: string;
    }): Promise<Result<string | null>>;
    listProducts(input: CatalogueQuery): Promise<Result<Page<ProductView>>>;
    getProduct(input: SessionRequest & {
        readonly productId: string;
    }): Promise<Result<ProductView>>;
    scanProduct(input: SessionRequest & {
        readonly barcode: string;
    }): Promise<Result<ProductView | null>>;
    createProduct(input: ProductRequest): Promise<Result<ProductView>>;
    updateProduct(input: ProductUpdateRequest): Promise<Result<ProductView>>;
    setProductActive(input: SessionRequest & {
        readonly productId: string;
        readonly version: string;
        readonly active: boolean;
    }): Promise<Result<ProductView>>;
    productPhoto(input: SessionRequest & {
        readonly productId: string;
    }): Promise<Result<PhotoView | null>>;
    setProductPhoto(input: SessionRequest & {
        readonly productId: string;
        readonly bytes: Uint8Array;
    }): Promise<Result<ProductView>>;
    removeProductPhoto(input: SessionRequest & {
        readonly productId: string;
    }): Promise<Result<ProductView>>;
    photoUsage(input: SessionRequest): Promise<Result<PhotoUsage>>;
    listCategories(input: CatalogueQuery): Promise<Result<Page<CategoryView>>>;
    createCategory(input: SessionRequest & {
        readonly requestId: string;
        readonly name: string;
    }): Promise<Result<CategoryView>>;
    updateCategory(input: SessionRequest & {
        readonly categoryId: string;
        readonly version: string;
        readonly name: string;
        readonly active: boolean;
    }): Promise<Result<CategoryView>>;
    moveStock(input: StockMoveRequest): Promise<Result<ProductView>>;
    countStock(input: StockCountRequest): Promise<Result<{
        readonly deltaThousandths: number;
        readonly product: ProductView;
    }>>;
    stockHistory(input: SessionRequest & {
        readonly productId: string;
        readonly after?: PageQuery["after"];
        readonly limit?: number;
    }): Promise<Result<Page<StockMovementView>>>;
    listPaymentAccounts(input: SessionRequest & PageQuery): Promise<Result<Page<PaymentAccountView>>>;
    listCustomers(input: CatalogueQuery): Promise<Result<Page<CustomerView>>>;
    getCustomer(input: SessionRequest & {
        readonly customerId: string;
    }): Promise<Result<CustomerView>>;
    createCustomer(input: SessionRequest & CustomerDetails & {
        readonly requestId: string;
    }): Promise<Result<CustomerView>>;
    updateCustomer(input: SessionRequest & CustomerDetails & {
        readonly customerId: string;
        readonly version: string;
        readonly active: boolean;
    }): Promise<Result<CustomerView>>;
    receiveCustomerPayment(input: SessionRequest & {
        readonly requestId: string;
        readonly customerId: string;
        readonly accountId: string;
        readonly amountMinor: number;
        readonly note?: string;
    }): Promise<Result<CustomerView>>;
    customerStatement(input: SessionRequest & {
        readonly customerId: string;
        readonly after?: PageQuery["after"];
        readonly limit?: number;
    }): Promise<Result<Page<StatementEntryView>>>;
    listSuppliers(input: CatalogueQuery): Promise<Result<Page<SupplierView>>>;
    getSupplier(input: SessionRequest & {
        readonly supplierId: string;
    }): Promise<Result<SupplierView>>;
    createSupplier(input: SessionRequest & SupplierDetails & {
        readonly requestId: string;
    }): Promise<Result<SupplierView>>;
    updateSupplier(input: SessionRequest & SupplierDetails & {
        readonly supplierId: string;
        readonly version: string;
        readonly active: boolean;
    }): Promise<Result<SupplierView>>;
    paySupplier(input: SessionRequest & {
        readonly requestId: string;
        readonly supplierId: string;
        readonly accountId: string;
        readonly amountMinor: number;
        readonly note?: string;
    }): Promise<Result<SupplierView>>;
    supplierStatement(input: SessionRequest & {
        readonly supplierId: string;
        readonly after?: PageQuery["after"];
        readonly limit?: number;
    }): Promise<Result<Page<StatementEntryView>>>;
    recordPurchase(input: PurchaseRequest): Promise<Result<{
        readonly purchaseId: string;
    }>>;
    listPurchases(input: SessionRequest & {
        readonly after?: PageQuery["after"];
        readonly limit?: number;
    }): Promise<Result<Page<PurchaseSummary>>>;
    getPurchase(input: SessionRequest & {
        readonly purchaseId: string;
    }): Promise<Result<PurchaseView>>;
    listAccounts(input: CatalogueQuery): Promise<Result<Page<AccountView>>>;
    createAccount(input: SessionRequest & {
        readonly requestId: string;
        readonly name: string;
        readonly kind: AccountKind;
    }): Promise<Result<AccountView>>;
    updateAccount(input: SessionRequest & {
        readonly accountId: string;
        readonly version: string;
        readonly name: string;
        readonly kind: AccountKind;
        readonly active: boolean;
    }): Promise<Result<AccountView>>;
    moveMoney(input: SessionRequest & {
        readonly requestId: string;
        readonly accountId: string;
        readonly direction: "in" | "out";
        readonly amountMinor: number;
        readonly reason: string;
    }): Promise<Result<AccountView>>;
    transferMoney(input: SessionRequest & {
        readonly requestId: string;
        readonly fromAccountId: string;
        readonly toAccountId: string;
        readonly amountMinor: number;
        readonly reason: string;
    }): Promise<Result<AccountView>>;
    countCash(input: SessionRequest & {
        readonly accountId: string;
        readonly countedMinor: number;
    }): Promise<Result<CashCountView>>;
    correctCash(input: SessionRequest & {
        readonly requestId: string;
        readonly accountId: string;
        readonly countedMinor: number;
        readonly reason: string;
    }): Promise<Result<CashCountView>>;
    listExpenses(input: SessionRequest & {
        readonly after?: PageQuery["after"];
        readonly limit?: number;
    }): Promise<Result<Page<ExpenseView>>>;
    recordExpense(input: SessionRequest & {
        readonly requestId: string;
        readonly accountId: string;
        readonly category: ExpenseCategory;
        readonly amountMinor: number;
        readonly description?: string;
        readonly reference?: string;
    }): Promise<Result<ExpenseView>>;
    reverseExpense(input: SessionRequest & {
        readonly requestId: string;
        readonly expenseId: string;
    }): Promise<Result<ExpenseView>>;
    report(input: SessionRequest & {
        readonly kind: ReportKind;
        readonly from: string;
        readonly until: string;
    }): Promise<Result<ReportResult>>;
    analytics(input: SessionRequest & {
        readonly from: string;
        readonly until: string;
        readonly offsetMinutes: number;
        readonly bucket?: AnalyticsBucket;
    }): Promise<Result<AnalyticsResult>>;
    journal(input: SessionRequest & {
        readonly from?: string;
        readonly until?: string;
        readonly actorId?: string;
        readonly deviceId?: string;
        readonly group?: AuditGroup;
        readonly subjectId?: string;
        readonly after?: PageQuery["after"];
        readonly limit?: number;
    }): Promise<Result<AuditPage>>;
    journalExport(input: SessionRequest & {
        readonly from?: string;
        readonly until?: string;
        readonly actorId?: string;
        readonly deviceId?: string;
        readonly group?: AuditGroup;
        readonly subjectId?: string;
    }): Promise<Result<{
        readonly items: readonly AuditEntry[];
        readonly truncated: boolean;
    }>>;
    heldBack(input: SessionRequest): Promise<Result<readonly HeldBackFact[]>>;
    reviewHeldBack(input: SessionRequest & {
        readonly eventId: string;
        readonly digest: string;
    }): Promise<Result<{
        readonly outcome: string;
    }>>;
    saveCsv(input: SessionRequest & {
        readonly filename: string;
        readonly content: string;
    }): Promise<Result<boolean>>;
    saveReportPdf(input: SessionRequest & {
        readonly kind: ReportKind;
        readonly from: string;
        readonly until: string;
        readonly filename: string;
        readonly text: Omit<ReportDocumentText, "shop">;
    }): Promise<Result<boolean>>;
    returnSource(input: SessionRequest & {
        readonly kind: "SALE" | "PURCHASE";
        readonly originalId: string;
    }): Promise<Result<ReturnSourceView>>;
    previewReturn(input: SessionRequest & {
        readonly kind: "SALE" | "PURCHASE";
        readonly originalId: string;
        readonly items: ReturnRequest["items"];
    }): Promise<Result<ReturnPreview>>;
    recordReturn(input: ReturnRequest): Promise<Result<{
        readonly returnId: string;
    }>>;
    listStaff(input: SessionRequest): Promise<Result<readonly StaffView[]>>;
    createStaff(input: SessionRequest & {

    createCustomRole(input: SessionRequest & {

    renameCustomRole(input: SessionRequest & {

    setCustomRolePermission(input: SessionRequest & {

        readonly permission: Permission;

    setStaffActive(input: SessionRequest & {

    resetStaffSecret(input: SessionRequest & {

    saveBackup(input: SessionRequest & {

    chooseImportFile(): Promise<Result<{

    openBackup(input: {

    backupKeys(input: SessionRequest): Promise<Result<readonly BackupKeyView[]>>;

    discardFile(): Promise<Result<null>>;
    saveInvite(input: SessionRequest & {

    openPairingRequest(input: SessionRequest): Promise<Result<PairingRequestView>>;
```
