
One release's notes as GitHub renders them (HTML); the renderer shows their text only.

```ts
export interface ReleaseNote {
    readonly version: string;
    readonly html: string;
}
```

A newer version: its notes for every release since this one, and whether it may break what this version does.

```ts
export interface FoundUpdate {
    readonly version: string;
    readonly notes: readonly ReleaseNote[];
    readonly breaking: boolean;
}
```

Where an update stands on this computer; failures carry no detail beyond the step that failed.

```ts
export type UpdateStatus = {
    readonly state: "idle";
} | {
    readonly state: "checking";
} | {
    readonly state: "current";
    readonly checkedAt: string;
} | ({
    readonly state: "available";
} & FoundUpdate) | ({
    readonly state: "downloading";
    readonly percent: number;
} & FoundUpdate) | ({
    readonly state: "ready";
} & FoundUpdate) | {
    readonly state: "failed";
    readonly step: "check" | "download" | "backup" | "install";
};
```

This computer's update state and preference.

```ts
export interface UpdateView {
    readonly currentVersion: string;
    readonly mode: "install" | "notify" | "none";
    readonly automatic: boolean;
    readonly installsOnClose: boolean;
    readonly status: UpdateStatus;
}
```

Desktop application updates; a per-computer concern outside any shop, so no session is needed.

```ts
export interface UpdateService {
    updateState(): Promise<Result<UpdateView>>;
    checkForUpdate(): Promise<Result<UpdateView>>;
    downloadUpdate(): Promise<Result<UpdateView>>;
    installUpdate(): Promise<Result<UpdateView>>;
    setAutomaticUpdates(input: {
        readonly enabled: boolean;
    }): Promise<Result<UpdateView>>;
    openDownloads(): Promise<Result<null>>;
    onUpdate(listener: (view: UpdateView) => void): () => void;
}
```

## [apps/server/src/admin/accounts.ts](../../apps/server/src/admin/accounts.ts)

Operator roles: `admin` changes things, `viewer` only reads.

```ts
export type AdminRole = "admin" | "viewer";
```

The signed-in operator, resolved from a session cookie on every request.

```ts
export interface Admin {
    readonly adminId: string;
    readonly username: string;
    readonly role: AdminRole;
}
```

A resolved dashboard session: who, which session (by token hash), and when it was last confirmed (step-up).

```ts
export interface AdminSession {
    readonly admin: Admin;
    readonly tokenHash: string;
    readonly expiresAt: Date;
    readonly confirmedAt: Date | null;
}
```

Who wrote an audit entry; the CLI writes as `cli` with no ID.

```ts
export interface AuditActor {
    readonly adminId: string | null;
    readonly username: string;
    readonly address?: string | null;
}
```

Stable admin failure; the code is the whole client-visible explanation.

```ts
class AdminError extends Error {
  status: number;
  code: string;
  constructor(status: number, code: string);
}
```

Session lifetimes: idle sign-out and an absolute cap.

```ts
const ADMIN_IDLE_MINUTES: 30;
const ADMIN_SESSION_HOURS: 12;
```

How long a step-up confirmation lets a session make sensitive changes (ADR-045).

```ts
const STEP_UP_MINUTES: 5;
```

Rejects passwords an operator could guess or mistype into something weak.

```ts
validatePassword(password: string): void;
```

A random 24-character password for a new operator, shown once.

```ts
generatePassword(): string;
```

`scrypt$N$r$p$salt$key`, so parameters can be raised later without breaking existing hashes.

```ts
hashPassword(password: string): Promise<string>;
```

A new operator: password hash, a fresh TOTP secret and its `otpauth://` URI for an authenticator app.

```ts
createAdmin(db: Kysely<ServerDatabase>, input: {
    readonly username: string;
    readonly password: string;
    readonly role: AdminRole;
}, actor: AuditActor = CLI): Promise<{ readonly adminId: string; readonly username: string; readonly secret: string; readonly uri: string; }>;
```

Replaces an operator's authenticator secret, for a lost phone, by username or ID. Unlocks the account and signs every session out.

```ts
resetTotp(db: Kysely<ServerDatabase>, who: string | {
    readonly adminId: string;
}, actor: AuditActor = CLI): Promise<{ readonly username: string; readonly secret: string; readonly uri: string; }>;
```

Password plus a fresh TOTP code (or the password alone where the operator turned TOTP off on a loopback-only
dashboard). Repeated failures lock the account for a while; a code is accepted once only.
Every outcome is audited. Returns a session token for an HttpOnly cookie; only its hash is stored.

```ts
signIn(db: Kysely<ServerDatabase>, input: {
    readonly username: string;
    readonly password: string;
} & Credentials): Promise<{ readonly token: string; readonly admin: Admin; }>;
```

Step-up (ADR-045): the signed-in operator re-enters their password and a fresh authenticator code, which marks this
session confirmed for `STEP_UP_MINUTES`. Failures count toward the same lockout as sign-in.

```ts
confirmSession(db: Kysely<ServerDatabase>, session: AdminSession, input: {
    readonly password: string;
} & Credentials): Promise<void>;
```

Refuses a sensitive change unless this session was confirmed within `STEP_UP_MINUTES`.

```ts
requireConfirmed(session: AdminSession, now: Date): void;
```

Resolves a session token, enforcing idle and absolute expiry and that the account is still active. With `touch`
false (a page refreshing itself in the background) the session is not kept alive, so an unattended dashboard still
signs out after `ADMIN_IDLE_MINUTES`.

```ts
resolveSession(db: Kysely<ServerDatabase>, token: string | undefined, now: Date, touch = true): Promise<AdminSession>;
```

The operator behind a session token; see `resolveSession`.

```ts
sessionAdmin(db: Kysely<ServerDatabase>, token: string | undefined, now: Date): Promise<Admin>;
```

Ends one session; idempotent.

```ts
signOut(db: Kysely<ServerDatabase>, token: string | undefined, admin: Admin, address: string): Promise<void>;
```

One of the operator's own live sessions; `id` is a prefix of the token hash, never the token.

```ts
export interface SessionView {
    readonly id: string;
    readonly createdAt: string;
    readonly lastSeenAt: string;
    readonly expiresAt: string;
    readonly address: string;
    readonly current: boolean;
}
```

The operator's live sessions, most recent first.

```ts
listSessions(db: Kysely<ServerDatabase>, session: AdminSession, now: Date): Promise<readonly SessionView[]>;
```

Ends one of the operator's own sessions by its listed ID, or every other one with `"others"`. Returns how many ended.

```ts
revokeSessions(db: Kysely<ServerDatabase>, session: AdminSession, which: string, address: string): Promise<number>;
```

Replaces the operator's own password and signs their other sessions out. Requires a confirmed session.

```ts
changePassword(db: Kysely<ServerDatabase>, session: AdminSession, password: string, address: string): Promise<void>;
```

An operator account as listed to admins; never secrets or hashes.

```ts
export interface OperatorView {
    readonly adminId: string;
    readonly username: string;
    readonly role: AdminRole;
    readonly active: boolean;
    readonly locked: boolean;
    readonly createdAt: string;
    readonly lastLoginAt: string | null;
    readonly sessions: number;
}
```

Every operator account, by username.

```ts
listOperators(db: Kysely<ServerDatabase>, now: Date): Promise<readonly OperatorView[]>;
```

A change to another operator's account.

```ts
export type OperatorChange = {
    readonly kind: "deactivate";
} | {
    readonly kind: "activate";
} | {
    readonly kind: "unlock";
} | {
    readonly kind: "role";
    readonly role: AdminRole;
};
```

Changes another operator's account, audited as `admin.<kind>`. An operator never changes their own account here
(`self_change`), and the last active admin can be neither deactivated nor demoted (`last_admin`): the active admin
rows are locked first, so two parallel changes cannot both pass the count. Deactivating signs the account out.
A change that is already in effect does nothing and records nothing.

```ts
changeOperator(db: Kysely<ServerDatabase>, actor: AuditActor & {
    readonly adminId: string;
}, adminId: string, change: OperatorChange): Promise<void>;
```

Appends one audit entry inside the caller's transaction, so a change and its record commit together. The database chains it (ADR-045).

```ts
audit(tx: Transaction<ServerDatabase> | Kysely<ServerDatabase>, admin: {
    readonly adminId: string | null;
    readonly username: string;
}, action: string, target: string | null, address: string | null, detail: Record<string, unknown> | null): Promise<void>;
```

## [apps/server/src/admin/admin-app.ts](../../apps/server/src/admin/admin-app.ts)

Everything the operator dashboard needs; it shares the sync server's database, identity and counters.

```ts
export interface AdminOptions {
    readonly db: Kysely<ServerDatabase>;
    readonly key: ServerKey;
    readonly publicUrl: string | undefined;
    readonly metrics: Metrics;
    readonly version: string;
    readonly uiDir: string | undefined;
    readonly trustProxy?: boolean;
    readonly rateLimitMax?: number;
    readonly now?: () => Date;
    readonly edition?: Edition;
    readonly totp?: boolean;
}
```

The operator dashboard: a JSON API under `/admin/api` and the static dashboard under `/admin/`, served on its own
listener so the public sync port never exposes it. Sessions use an HttpOnly, SameSite=Strict cookie; every change
also requires a matching `Origin`. Viewers can read only. Every change is audited in its own transaction. Requests
are checked against strict schemas after the session is (ADR-045), and the most sensitive changes need a step-up
confirmation from the last `STEP_UP_MINUTES`.

```ts
createAdminApplication(options: AdminOptions): Promise<FastifyInstance<RawServerDefault, IncomingMessage, ServerResponse<IncomingMessage>, FastifyBaseLogger, FastifyTypeProviderDefault>>;
```

## [apps/server/src/admin/operations.ts](../../apps/server/src/admin/operations.ts)

Who performed a change and from where; the CLI acts as `cli`.

```ts
export interface Actor {
    readonly adminId: string | null;
    readonly username: string;
    readonly address: string | null;
}
```

The CLI's identity in the audit log.

```ts
const CLI_ACTOR: Actor;
```

Days a removed shop is kept, restorable, before its data is purged.

```ts
const REMOVAL_GRACE_DAYS: 30;
```

A freshly issued connection code. `code` is shown once; only the token's hash is stored.

```ts
export interface IssuedCode {
    readonly codeId: string;
    readonly code: string;
    readonly label: string;
    readonly expiresAt: string;
}
```

A connection code as listed, never with its token.

```ts
export interface CodeView {
    readonly codeId: string;
    readonly label: string;
    readonly createdBy: string;
    readonly createdAt: string;
    readonly expiresAt: string;
    readonly state: "unused" | "used" | "expired" | "cancelled";
    readonly usedAt: string | null;
    readonly shopId: string | null;
    readonly shopName: string | null;
    readonly includedMonths: number;
    readonly includedDays: number;
    readonly maxTills: number | null;
    readonly recoveryShopId: string | null;
}
```

Issues a single-use connection code for one shop. The string carries the server's public address, its fingerprint
and a 128-bit token, so the owner pastes or scans one thing and never retypes a fingerprint (ADR-030).
With `recoveryShopId` it is an owner-recovery code (ADR-036): it can only attach a restored owner device to that
existing shop after every till was lost, and never creates a shop. Issue one only after confirming the owner.
An ordinary code may carry paid months and days (a trial) and the shop's till limit (`maxTills`, null unlimited;
ADR-039).

```ts
issueCode(db: Kysely<ServerDatabase>, key: ServerKey, publicUrl: string, actor: Actor, input: {
    readonly label: string;
    readonly days: number;
    readonly now: Date;
    readonly includedMonths?: number;
    readonly includedDays?: number;
    readonly maxTills?: number | null;
    readonly recoveryShopId?: string;
}): Promise<IssuedCode>;
```

What became of a code.

```ts
const CODE_STATES: readonly ["unused", "used", "expired", "cancelled"];
```

```ts
export type CodeState = typeof CODE_STATES[number];
```

Codes, newest first, with what became of each; optionally one state, a page at a time.

```ts
listCodes(db: Kysely<ServerDatabase>, now: Date, query: {
    readonly state?: CodeState;
    readonly offset?: number;
    readonly limit?: number;
} = {}): Promise<{ readonly codes: readonly CodeView[]; readonly total: number; }>;
```

Cancels an unused code. A used code has done its job and cannot be cancelled.

```ts
cancelCode(db: Kysely<ServerDatabase>, actor: Actor, codeId: string, now: Date): Promise<void>;
```

A shop as the operator sees it: identity, status and sync health, never business records.

```ts
export interface ShopView {
    readonly shopId: string;
    readonly name: string;
    readonly status: "active" | "suspended" | "removed";
    readonly enrolledAt: string;
    readonly removeAfter: string | null;
    readonly devices: number;
    readonly lastSeenAt: string | null;
    readonly facts: number;
    readonly bytes: number;
    readonly service: "unlimited" | "active" | "grace" | "inactive";
    readonly paidUntil: string | null;
    readonly maxTills: number | null;
    readonly heldTills: number;
}
```

Orders the shop list can take.

```ts
const SHOP_SORTS: readonly ["lastSeen", "name", "enrolled", "facts", "bytes", "paidUntil"];
```

```ts
export type ShopSort = typeof SHOP_SORTS[number];
```

Filters, order and page for `listShops`. `search` matches the name, or an ID prefix when it looks like one.

```ts
export interface ShopQuery {
    readonly search?: string;
    readonly shopId?: string;
    readonly status?: ShopView["status"];
    readonly service?: "active" | "grace" | "inactive";
    readonly sort?: ShopSort;
    readonly dir?: "asc" | "desc";
    readonly offset?: number;
    readonly limit?: number;
}
```

Shops matching `query`, most recently active first by default, with the total that match.

```ts
listShops(db: Kysely<ServerDatabase>, query: ShopQuery = {}, edition: Edition = { edition: "selfhost", graceDays: 0 }, now = new Date()): Promise<{ readonly shops: readonly ShopView[]; readonly total: number; }>;
```

One shop's devices and delivery health; the key epoch is read from sealed headers, never from content.

```ts
export interface ShopDetail extends ShopView {
    readonly ownerId: string;
    readonly conflicts: number;
    readonly keyEpoch: number | null;
    readonly connectionCode: string | null;
    readonly graceUntil: string | null;
    readonly payments: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof listPayments>>;
    readonly activity: readonly AuditView[];
    readonly deviceList: readonly {
        readonly deviceId: string;
        readonly label: string;
        readonly platform: string;
        readonly active: boolean;
        readonly held: boolean;
        readonly authority: boolean;
        readonly lastSeenAt: string | null;
        readonly backlog: number;
    }[];
}
```

One shop as the operator sees it, with its tills, payments and recent audit entries.

```ts
shopDetail(db: Kysely<ServerDatabase>, shopId: string, edition: Edition = { edition: "selfhost", graceDays: 0 }, now = new Date()): Promise<ShopDetail>;
```

Changes a shop's status. Suspended and removed shops are refused on their next sync request while their tills keep
trading; removal is a soft delete restorable for `REMOVAL_GRACE_DAYS`, after which `purgeRemovedShops` deletes it.

```ts
setShopStatus(db: Kysely<ServerDatabase>, actor: Actor, shopId: string, action: "suspend" | "resume" | "remove" | "restore", now: Date): Promise<void>;
```

Sets a shop's till limit (null for unlimited), audited as `shop.till_limit`. Raising or clearing it releases held
tills, oldest first, into the new places; lowering it below current use holds nobody (ADR-039). Returns the
released device IDs. With `expected`, the change is refused (`stale`) unless the limit is still that value, so two
operators editing at once cannot silently overwrite each other.

```ts
setTillLimit(db: Kysely<ServerDatabase>, actor: Actor, shopId: string, maxTills: number | null, expected?: number | null): Promise<readonly string[]>;
```

Records a payment taken by the operator and moves the shop's paid-until date; audited in the same transaction.
`requestId` (a UUID the form generates once) makes a retried or double-submitted request record the payment once:
the replay returns the original and is not audited again.

```ts
recordShopPayment(db: Kysely<ServerDatabase>, actor: Actor, input: {
    readonly shopId: string;
    readonly months: number;
    readonly amountMinor: number;
    readonly method: PaymentMethod;
    readonly reference?: string;
    readonly requestId?: string;
}, now: Date): Promise<RecordedPayment>;
```

Deletes every row of shops whose removal grace period has passed. This is the only deletion of append-only rows,
allowed by a transaction-local setting that names the shop being purged. Returns the purged shop IDs.

```ts
purgeRemovedShops(db: Kysely<ServerDatabase>, actor: Actor, now: Date): Promise<readonly string[]>;
```

In-process request counters since start, shared by the public listener and the dashboard.

```ts
export interface Metrics {
    readonly startedAt: Date;
    requests: number;
    clientErrors: number;
    serverErrors: number;
    rateLimited: number;
}
```

```ts
createMetrics(now = new Date()): Metrics;
```

Server-wide health for the dashboard, with the runtime it runs on.

```ts
serverHealth(db: Kysely<ServerDatabase>, metrics: Metrics, version: string, now: Date, edition: Edition = { edition: "selfhost", graceDays: 0 }): Promise<{ version: string; edition: "selfhost" | "hosted"; graceDays: number; startedAt: string; databaseBytes: number; facts: number; factsLastDay: number; activeSessions: number; shops: { active: number; suspended: number; removed: number; }; ... 5 more ...; runtime: { ...; }; }>;
```

An audit entry as listed; `seq` is its place in the hash chain and `hash` its link (ADR-045).

```ts
export interface AuditView {
    readonly id: string;
    readonly seq: string;
    readonly hash: string;
    readonly at: string;
    readonly username: string;
    readonly action: string;
    readonly target: string | null;
    readonly address: string | null;
    readonly detail: Record<string, unknown> | null;
}
```

Filters for `listAudit`: `action` is a prefix (`shop.` matches every shop change), the others exact.

```ts
export interface AuditQuery {
    readonly before?: string;
    readonly action?: string;
    readonly username?: string;
    readonly target?: string;
    readonly from?: Date;
    readonly to?: Date;
    readonly limit?: number;
}
```

Audit entries, newest first, filtered, optionally before an ID for paging.

```ts
listAudit(db: Kysely<ServerDatabase>, query: AuditQuery = {}): Promise<readonly AuditView[]>;
```

Something the operator should look at, linking to its shop or code.

```ts
export interface AttentionItem {
    readonly kind: "service_inactive" | "service_grace" | "service_expiring" | "held_tills" | "conflicts" | "stale_shop" | "code_expiring" | "purge_soon" | "server_errors" | "integrity";
    readonly target: string | null;
    readonly label: string;
    readonly at: string | null;
    readonly count: number | null;
}
```

Days without any till contact before an active shop is called stale.

```ts
const STALE_SHOP_DAYS: 14;
```

The dashboard's first page: counts across the server, facts received per UTC day for the last 14 days, hosted
revenue for 30 days, and what needs attention. Reads only; each attention kind is capped at 20 items.

```ts
overview(db: Kysely<ServerDatabase>, metrics: Metrics, edition: Edition, now: Date): Promise<{ edition: "selfhost" | "hosted"; shops: { active: number; suspended: number; removed: number; }; services: { active: number; grace: number; inactive: number; }; tills: { used: number; held: number; }; ... 4 more ...; attention: AttentionItem[]; }>;
```

Hosted billing across shops (ADR-031): payments recorded in [`from`, `to`), newest first and paged, their totals by
method and by source, and the subscriptions that end within 30 days or have already lapsed. Refused on self-hosted servers.

```ts
billingReport(db: Kysely<ServerDatabase>, edition: Edition, now: Date, query: {
    readonly from: Date;
    readonly to: Date;
    readonly offset?: number;
    readonly limit?: number;
}): Promise<{ from: string; to: string; payments: { paymentId: string; shopId: string; shopName: string; months: number; days: number; amountMinor: number; method: "other" | "cash" | "mtn_momo" | "orange_money" | "bank" | "card"; ... 4 more ...; paidUntilAfter: string; }[]; total: { ...; }; byMethod: { ...; }[]; bySourc...;
```

One consistency check: `targets` names what fails it (shop IDs, or the first broken audit `seq`), at most 20.

```ts
export interface IntegrityCheck {
    readonly name: "audit_chain" | "payment_chain" | "paid_until" | "held_with_free_places" | "sessions_on_stopped_shops" | "purge_overdue";
    readonly ok: boolean;
    readonly targets: readonly string[];
}
```

Read-only consistency checks over what the server guarantees by construction (ADR-045): the audit hash chain
recomputes and links from the first entry to the head; each shop's payments chain (every payment starts where the
previous ended) and its paid-until date is the last one's end; no till waits while a place is free; no sync session
outlives a suspension or removal; nothing removed waits more than two hours past its purge date. The audit head is
returned so an operator can note it and later notice a rewritten or truncated chain.

```ts
integrityReport(db: Kysely<ServerDatabase>, now: Date): Promise<{ ok: boolean; checkedAt: string; checks: IntegrityCheck[]; audit: { entries: number; headSeq: string; headHash: string; }; }>;
```

```ts
        readonly code: string;

}
```

```ts
    readonly key?: ServerKey;

}
```

## [apps/server/src/billing.ts](../../apps/server/src/billing.ts)

How this deployment bills: self-hosted servers never expire a shop.

```ts
export interface Edition {
    readonly edition: "selfhost" | "hosted";
    readonly graceDays: number;
}
```

Payment methods recorded by the operator or a provider.

```ts
export type PaymentMethod = "cash" | "mtn_momo" | "orange_money" | "bank" | "card" | "other";
```

```ts
const PAYMENT_METHODS: readonly PaymentMethod[];
```

Stable billing refusal; the code is the whole client-visible explanation.

```ts
class BillingError extends Error {
  status: number;
  code: string;
  constructor(status: number, code: string);
}
```

Hosted service status for a paid-until date: `active` while paid, `grace` for `graceDays` after, then `inactive`.
A shop never paid for is `inactive`. Self-hosted shops are `unlimited`. Local trading never depends on any of it.

```ts
entitlementOf(edition: Edition, paidUntil: Date | null, now: Date): { edition: "selfhost" | "hosted"; status: "active" | "inactive" | "grace" | "unlimited"; paid_until?: string; grace_until?: string; };
```

A payment to record: one plan per shop, so only the paid time matters for service. Manual and webhook payments are
whole months; a code payment may also, or only, carry days (a trial, ADR-039).

```ts
export interface PaymentInput {
    readonly shopId: string;
    readonly months: number;
    readonly days?: number;
    readonly amountMinor: number;
    readonly method: PaymentMethod;
    readonly reference?: string | undefined;
    readonly source: "manual" | "webhook" | "code";
    readonly externalId?: string | undefined;
    readonly recordedBy: string;
}
```

The stored effect of a payment: the paid-until date before and after it.

```ts
export interface RecordedPayment {
    readonly paymentId: string;
    readonly paidUntilBefore: string | null;
    readonly paidUntilAfter: string;
    readonly duplicate: boolean;
}
```

Records a payment and moves the shop's paid-until date forward by whole calendar months (then any days), from today
if it had lapsed or from its current end otherwise, in the caller's transaction. A repeated `externalId` (a provider retrying
its webhook) changes nothing and returns the original payment.

```ts
recordPayment(tx: Transaction<ServerDatabase>, input: PaymentInput, now: Date): Promise<RecordedPayment>;
```

A shop's payments, newest first.

```ts
listPayments(db: Kysely<ServerDatabase>, shopId: string): Promise<{ paymentId: string; months: number; days: number; amountMinor: number; method: "other" | "cash" | "mtn_momo" | "orange_money" | "bank" | "card"; reference: string; source: "manual" | "webhook" | "code"; recordedBy: string; recordedAt: string; paidUntilAfter: string; }[]>;
```

A payment provider's webhook, reduced to what kolo needs. Each provider gets one adapter that verifies its own
signature scheme and maps its payload; nothing else in the server knows which provider is used.

```ts
export interface BillingAdapter {
    readonly name: string;
    verify(rawBody: string, headers: Readonly<Record<string, string | string[] | undefined>>, now: Date): Omit<PaymentInput, "source" | "recordedBy">;
}
```

The provider-neutral adapter: `x-kolo-signature: t=<unix seconds>,v1=<hex HMAC-SHA256 of "<t>.<raw body>">` with the
shared secret, accepted within five minutes. Body: `{ event_id, shop_id, months, amount_minor, method, reference? }`.
A small relay in front of any provider (or the provider itself) can send this.

```ts
genericWebhookAdapter(secret: string): BillingAdapter;
```

Typed PostgreSQL schema. Every business table is keyed by `business_id`; repositories always bind it.

```ts
export interface ServerDatabase {
    shops: {
        business_id: string;
        name: string;
        owner_id: string;
        enrolled_by: string;
        enrolled_at: Generated<Date>;
        status: Generated<"active" | "suspended" | "removed">;

        max_tills: number | null;

        business_id: string;
        device_id: string;
        public_key: string;
        label: string;

        authority_allowed: boolean;
        active: boolean;
        roster_revision: Generated<string>;
        updated_at: Generated<Date>;
        last_seen_at: Date | null;
        held: Generated<boolean>;
    };
    challenges: {
        challenge_id: string;
        purpose: "enrollment" | "session";
        business_id: string;
        device_id: string;
        nonce: string;

    };
    sessions: {
        token_hash: string;
        business_id: string;
        device_id: string;
        created_at: Generated<Date>;
        expires_at: Date;
    };
    journal: {
        seq: Generated<string>;
        business_id: string;
        event_id: string;
        origin_device_id: string;
        origin_sequence: string;
        event_type: string | null;
        event_version: number | null;
        canonical: string | null;
        digest: string;
        signature: string | null;
        sealed: string | null;
        subject_device_id: string | null;
        roster_revision: string | null;
        received_from: string;
        received_at: Generated<Date>;
        vouched: Generated<boolean>;
    };
    journal_conflicts: {
        id: Generated<string>;
        business_id: string;
        event_id: string;
        digest: string;
        canonical: string | null;
        signature: string | null;
        sealed: string | null;
        received_from: string;
        detected_at: Generated<Date>;
    };
    push_batches: {
        business_id: string;
        batch_id: string;
        sender_device_id: string;

    };
    offered_batches: {

    receipts: {

        receipt_id: string;
        device_id: string;
        digest: string;
        canonical: string;
        signature: string;
        received_at: Generated<Date>;
    };
    connection_codes: {
        code_id: string;
        token_hash: string;
        label: string;
        created_by: string;
        created_at: Generated<Date>;
        expires_at: Date;
        used_at: Date | null;
        used_by: string | null;
        cancelled_at: Date | null;

        max_tills: number | null;
        included_days: Generated<number>;
    };
    join_sessions: {
        join_id: string;
        business_id: string;
        owner_device_id: string;
        offer: unknown;
        request: unknown | null;
        enrollment: string | null;
        sealed_password: unknown | null;
        created_at: Generated<Date>;
        expires_at: Date;
    };
    payments: {
        payment_id: string;
        business_id: string;
        months: number;
        days: Generated<number>;
        amount_minor: string;
        currency: "XAF";
        method: "cash" | "mtn_momo" | "orange_money" | "bank" | "card" | "other";
        reference: string | null;
        source: "manual" | "webhook" | "code";

}
```

Forward-only migrations, embedded so a deployed build carries the schema it expects. Never edit a shipped one.

```ts
const SERVER_MIGRATIONS: Readonly<Record<string, Migration>>;
```

Opens a bounded pool with connection and statement deadlines.

```ts
openDatabase(url: string): Kysely<ServerDatabase>;
```

Applies pending migrations in order inside Kysely's migration lock; refuses unknown newer ones. `discardPlaintext`
lets migration 0002 delete F6a plaintext business facts; it is set on the one connection the migrations run on.

```ts
migrateToLatest(db: Kysely<ServerDatabase>, options: {
    readonly discardPlaintext?: boolean;
} = {}): Promise<readonly string[]>;
```

True only when every embedded migration has been applied.

```ts
schemaCurrent(db: Kysely<ServerDatabase>): Promise<boolean>;
```

## [apps/server/src/identity.ts](../../apps/server/src/identity.ts)

The server's own device identity: one Ed25519 key for every shop it serves.

```ts
export interface ServerKey {
    readonly deviceId: string;
    readonly publicKey: string;
    readonly fingerprint: string;
    sign(message: string): string;
}
```

Lowercase hex SHA-256 of UTF-8 text.

```ts
sha256(message: string): string;
```

Verifies a detached Ed25519 signature under an SPKI PEM key; malformed keys or signatures are simply invalid.

```ts
verifySignature(publicKey: string, message: string, signatureHex: string): boolean;
```

Short key fingerprint, identical to the one a device computes before pinning the server.

```ts
fingerprintOf(publicKey: string): string;
```

A fresh in-memory identity, for tests and first start.

```ts
generateServerKey(): { key: ServerKey; privateKeyPem: string; };
```

Loads the identity file, or creates it exclusively with owner-only permissions on first start.
Losing this file changes the server's identity: every device must then confirm it again.

```ts
loadServerKey(path: string): ServerKey;
```

## [apps/server/src/sync.fixtures.ts](../../apps/server/src/sync.fixtures.ts)

Test-only fixtures for real PostgreSQL and real SQLite tills; only the network address is local.

```ts
const adminUrl: string;
```

Which PostgreSQL the tests run against: a real server from `KOLO_TEST_DATABASE_URL` (the authoritative gate,
`npm run test:server:pg`), otherwise in-process PGlite (PostgreSQL compiled to WASM) behind its wire-protocol socket,
so the suite also runs in every `npm test`. PGlite is one engine connection multiplexed across clients and is
PostgreSQL 17, so concurrency and version behaviour is only proven by the real-server run. A test that cannot hold
on PGlite should skip with `engine === "pglite"` rather than be weakened; none needs to today.

```ts
const engine: "postgres" | "pglite";
```

```ts
const at: "2026-10-11T06:00:00.000Z";
const secret: "fixture-owner-secret";
```

Node/OpenSSL implementation of the sealing port, as the desktop adapter does it.

```ts
const crypto: SealingCrypto;
```

```ts
export interface Device {
    db: Database;

    publicKey: string;
    privateKey: string;
    destination?: string;
}
```

```ts
    invite(label?: string, days?: number, months?: number, extra?: {
        readonly maxTills?: number | null;
        readonly includedDays?: number;
    }): Promise<{
        code: string;
        token: string;
    }>;
}
```

```ts
owner(name = "Fixture shop"): Promise<Device>;
```

A device paired with the owner's device only, the way F3 pairing leaves it: it knows the owner, nobody else.

```ts
paired(source: Device, label: string, authority = false): Promise<Device>;
```

A fresh, fully migrated PostgreSQL database per server; dropped after the run.

```ts
freshDatabase(): Promise<string>;
```

```ts
started(options: {
    rateLimitMax?: number;
    key?: ServerKey;
    now?: () => Date;
    edition?: "selfhost" | "hosted";
    graceDays?: number;
    webhookSecret?: string;
} = {}): Promise<Server>;
```

Owner's main till: a fresh single-use code. Any other till: the shared address only.

```ts
connect(device: Device, target: Server, token?: string): Promise<string>;
```

```ts
const sync: (device: Device, target: Server) => Promise<SyncSummary>;
```

```ts
const count: (device: Device, table: string) => number;
```

```ts
product(device: Device, name = "Rice"): string;
```

```ts
sale(device: Device, productId: string, number = `V-${randomUUIDv7()}`): string;
```

## [apps/server/src/sync/join.ts](../../apps/server/src/sync/join.ts)

Opens a mailbox for one join code (ADR-032). Only a shop's active authority till may, and the invite must be signed
by that till's own key for this shop, so a mailbox can never offer another shop or another till's invitation.

```ts
createJoin(relay: Relay, scope: Scope, offer: JoinOffer): Promise<{ expires_at: string; }>;
```

The offer for a code's hash; anyone may read it, and only someone holding the code can check or use it.

```ts
readJoin(relay: Relay, joinId: string): Promise<{ join_id: string; invite: { invite: { format: "kolo.pairing-invite"; format_version: 1; invite_id: string; business_id: string; business_name: string; owner_id: string; owner_name: string; source_device_id: string; ... 4 more ...; authority_allowed: boolean; }; digest: string; signature: string; }; mac: str...;
```

Stores the new till's request once; repeating the same request is harmless, a different one is refused.

```ts
requestJoin(relay: Relay, joinId: string, body: JoinRequestBody): Promise<void>;
```

The owner's till polls for the request.

```ts
joinRequestOf(relay: Relay, scope: Scope, joinId: string): Promise<{ request: { request: { format: "kolo.pairing-request"; format_version: 1; request_id: string; invite: { invite: { format: "kolo.pairing-invite"; format_version: 1; invite_id: string; business_id: string; ... 8 more ...; authority_allowed: boolean; }; digest: string; signature: string; }; ... 5 more ...; cre...;
```

The owner's approval: the encrypted shop file and its sealed password. The mailbox then waits ten more minutes.

```ts
enrollJoin(relay: Relay, scope: Scope, joinId: string, body: JoinEnrollment): Promise<void>;
```

The new till fetches its shop once approved; null while the owner has not answered.

```ts
joinEnrollmentOf(relay: Relay, joinId: string): Promise<{ enrollment: string; sealed_password: { ephemeral_public_key: string; nonce: string; ciphertext: string; tag: string; }; mac: string; }>;
```

The owner refuses or cancels; the new till then sees the code as gone.

```ts
cancelJoin(relay: Relay, scope: Scope, joinId: string): Promise<void>;
```

## [apps/server/src/sync/relay.ts](../../apps/server/src/sync/relay.ts)

Stable HTTP failure; the code is the whole client-visible explanation.

```ts
class RelayError extends Error {
  status: number;
  code: string;
  retry: boolean | undefined;
  constructor(status: number, code: string, retry?: boolean);
}
```

The authenticated network scope. Tenant and device always come from here, never from a request body.

```ts
export interface Scope {
    readonly businessId: string;
    readonly deviceId: string;
}
```

Shared relay dependencies.

```ts
export interface Relay {
    readonly db: Kysely<ServerDatabase>;
    readonly key: ServerKey;
    readonly sessionTtlSeconds: number;
    readonly now: () => Date;
    readonly edition?: Edition;
}
```

Issues a single-use challenge. It reveals nothing about whether the shop or device is known.

```ts
createChallenge(relay: Relay, input: ChallengeRequest): Promise<{ challenge_id: string; nonce: string; expires_at: string; }>;
```

Attaches an existing local shop, keeping its IDs. The claim must be signed by the device key it names, and a new
shop needs an unused, unexpired, uncancelled single-use connection code, which is consumed in the same transaction.
Re-enrolling from the same authority device is idempotent and needs no code, so a lost response is harmless. Another
device claiming an enrolled shop is a conflict, unless it brings an owner-recovery code issued for exactly that shop
and names its owner (ADR-036): the restored owner device is then added as an authority device, even when the shop
is at its till limit (ADR-039). A new shop starts with its code's till limit and paid months and days.

```ts
enroll(relay: Relay, input: EnrollmentRequest): Promise<void>;
```

This shop's hosted service status; available to a signed-in till even when sync is paused, so it can say why.

```ts
entitlement(relay: Relay, scope: Scope): Promise<{ edition: "selfhost" | "hosted"; status: "active" | "inactive" | "grace" | "unlimited"; paid_until?: string; grace_until?: string; }>;
```

Refuses data transfer for a hosted shop whose paid period and grace have ended. Its facts stay stored, its tills keep
trading, and everything waiting flows again once a payment is recorded.

```ts
requireService(relay: Relay, scope: Scope): Promise<void>;
```

Exchanges a signed challenge from an active device key for a short bearer token, stored only as a hash.

```ts
openSession(relay: Relay, input: SessionRequest): Promise<{ token: string; expires_at: string; business_id: string; device_id: string; server_device_id: string; }>;
```

Resolves a bearer token to its scope; a revoked device loses access on its next request.

```ts
authenticate(relay: Relay, header: string | undefined): Promise<Scope>;
```

Ends a network session; idempotent. Local history is untouched.

```ts
endSession(relay: Relay, header: string | undefined): Promise<void>;
```

Stores each fact durably and answers per event. Clear facts (trust, keys, server address) are verified against their
origin's key; sealed facts are accepted from the authenticated, batch-signing sender for any origin the shop's roster
knows, since only tills can open them and check their inner proof (ADR-030). Plain business facts are refused.
Unknown origins are retried within the batch, since an owner-signed trust fact may precede them in any order.
A signed receipt acknowledges exactly the stored and duplicate digests; nothing is applied to business tables here.

```ts
push(relay: Relay, scope: Scope, signed: SignedSyncBatch): Promise<{ results: { event_id: string; status: "stored" | "duplicate" | "rejected"; code?: "scope_mismatch" | "invalid_event" | "untrusted_device" | "invalid_signature" | "event_identity_conflict" | "plaintext_refused"; retry?: boolean; }[]; receipt?: { ...; }; }>;
```

A bounded, server-signed page of facts this device has not acknowledged, with every original proof intact. Each pass
offers the clear trust, key and address facts first, then the sealed ones, each oldest first: a new till cannot open
the sealed backlog until it holds its shop-key grant, so the grant must never wait behind it. The cursor only pages
within one pass; unacknowledged facts are offered again on the next pass from the start, so a fact skipped by the
device is never lost behind a cursor. A revoked device's facts are still offered: each till accepts them up to the
owner's cutoff and holds the rest for review (ADR-040), so revoking a till never silently drops its earlier sales.

```ts
pull(relay: Relay, scope: Scope, cursor: string | undefined, limit: number): Promise<{ has_more: boolean; batch?: { batch: { format: "kolo.sync-batch"; format_version: 1; batch_id: string; business_id: string; sender_device_id: string; receiver_device_id: string; created_at: string; events: ({ ...; } | { ...; })[]; }; digest: string; signature: string; }; cursor?: string; }>;
```

Owner-signed trust facts, so a device can trust the server's key before verifying a server-signed page.

```ts
bootstrap(relay: Relay, scope: Scope): Promise<{ events: { envelope: { event_id: string; business_id: string; origin_device_id: string; actor_id: string; origin_sequence: number; event_type: string; event_version: number; occurred_at: string; authority_revision: number; required_permissions: ("products.view" | ... 41 more ... | "data.export")[]; payload:...;
```

Records a device's signed acknowledgment of one offered page; applied never downgrades. Idempotent per receipt.

```ts
acceptReceipt(relay: Relay, scope: Scope, signed: SignedPeerReceipt): Promise<number>;
```

This device's backlog and the shop's visible conflicts.

```ts
status(relay: Relay, scope: Scope): Promise<{ pending: number; delivered: number; conflicts: number; devices: number; }>;
```

This shop's till limit and use (ADR-039); readable while sync is paused, so the till can say why a till waits.

```ts
plan(relay: Relay, scope: Scope): Promise<{ tills: number; max_tills?: number; }>;
```

## [apps/server/src/sync/tills.ts](../../apps/server/src/sync/tills.ts)

A shop's till limit and use (ADR-039). `max` null is unlimited.

```ts
export interface TillPlace {
    readonly max: number | null;
    readonly used: number;
}
```

Tills that count against the limit: active, not held, and not the server's own device.

```ts
tillsInUse(db: Db, businessId: string): Promise<number>;
```

The shop's limit and use. With `lock`, the shop row is locked first, so everything that adds or frees a place in the
same transaction sees one consistent count and two tills cannot both take the last place.

```ts
tillPlace(db: Db, businessId: string, lock = false): Promise<TillPlace>;
```

Whether one more till fits.

```ts
hasPlace(place: TillPlace): boolean;
```

Releases held tills, oldest first, into the places now free (after a revocation or a raised limit). The caller holds
the shop row lock. Returns the released device IDs.

```ts
releaseHeld(tx: Transaction<ServerDatabase>, businessId: string): Promise<readonly string[]>;
```

## [packages/contracts/src/canonical.ts](../../packages/contracts/src/canonical.ts)

Stable JSON representation of the bounded, safe-integer-only event format.

```ts
class CanonicalError extends Error {
  constructor();
}
```

Number of bytes when a valid Unicode string is encoded as UTF-8, without Buffer.

```ts
utf8Length(value: string): number;
```

RFC 8785 ordering/string serialization restricted to safe integer numbers.
Rejects non-JSON/prototype values, cycles, excessive depth/bytes and invalid
Unicode. Property names sort by UTF-16 code units; arrays retain order.
This determines signed bytes, not trust, a content digest or a signature.

```ts
canonicalJson(value: unknown, maximumBytes = 1024 * 1024): string;
```



```ts
export type JournalBatch = z.infer<typeof journalBatchSchema>;
```

```ts
export type SignedJournalBatch = z.infer<typeof signedJournalBatchSchema>;
```

```ts
export type PeerReceipt = z.infer<typeof peerReceiptSchema>;
```

```ts
export type SignedPeerReceipt = z.infer<typeof signedPeerReceiptSchema>;
```

Origin identity survives forwarding; server cursors are not event identity.

```ts

```

Legacy unsigned structural codec; conveys no authority. Use signed delivery and encrypted transfer formats for configured workspaces.


```ts
export type ExchangeBundle = z.infer<typeof exchangeBundleSchema>;
```

```ts
export type ContractProblem = "invalid_bundle" | "unsupported_version" | "another_business" | "too_large";
```

Stable boundary error. Messages contain no payloads, credentials, or customer data.

```ts
class ContractError extends Error {
  code: ContractProblem;
  constructor(code: ContractProblem);
}
```

Validate an untrusted decoded bundle against the receiving installation's shop.

```ts
parseExchangeBundle(input: unknown, expectedBusinessId: string): { format: "kolo.exchange"; format_version: 1; bundle_id: string; business_id: string; sender_device_id: string; created_at: string; events: { event_id: string; business_id: string; origin_device_id: string; ... 5 more ...; payload: Record<...>; }[]; };
```

Decode bounded UTF-8 JSON. Native file readers must also bound bytes before loading.

```ts
decodeExchangeBundle(source: string, expectedBusinessId: string): { format: "kolo.exchange"; format_version: 1; bundle_id: string; business_id: string; sender_device_id: string; created_at: string; events: { event_id: string; business_id: string; origin_device_id: string; ... 5 more ...; payload: Record<...>; }[]; };
```

Encode a structurally validated bundle. This does not sign it or acknowledge delivery.

```ts
encodeExchangeBundle(bundle: ExchangeBundle): string;
```

## [packages/contracts/src/permissions.ts](../../packages/contracts/src/permissions.ts)

Granular local permissions, retaining the archived server's role defaults.

```ts
const PERMISSIONS: readonly ["products.view", "products.create", "products.edit", "products.price", "products.archive", "inventory.view", "inventory.receive", "inventory.adjust", "inventory.count", "sales.create", "sales.view", "sales.view_own", "sales.refund", "sales.discount", "customers.view", "customers.manage", "customers.credit", "suppliers.view", "suppliers.manage", "purchases.view", "purchases.create", "purchases.pay", "expenses.view", "expenses.create", "finance.accounts.view", "finance.accounts.manage", "cash.view", "cash.count", "cash.reconcile", "reports.sales", "reports.inventory", "reports.customers", "reports.suppliers", "reports.financial", "users.view", "users.manage", "business.settings", "audit.view", "customers.pay", "customers.credit_override", "sales.price_override", "cash.move", "data.export"];
```

A known local capability; unknown strings are refused at runtime.

```ts
export type Permission = (typeof PERMISSIONS)[number];
```

Roles supply defaults; operations authorize individual permissions.

```ts
export type Role = "OWNER" | "MANAGER" | "CASHIER" | "INVENTORY" | "ACCOUNTANT";
```

A role the owner may assign and whose permissions the shop may edit (ADR-037); the owner always holds everything.

```ts
export type StaffRole = Exclude<Role, "OWNER">;
```

Assignable roles, in the order screens list them.

```ts
const STAFF_ROLES: readonly StaffRole[];
```

Recognizes a catalog entry without accepting arbitrary permission strings.

```ts
isPermission(value: unknown): boolean;
```

Recognizes an assignable role; `OWNER` and unknown strings are refused.

```ts
isStaffRole(value: unknown): boolean;
```

The built-in permissions of a role, used whenever the shop has not edited that role.

```ts
defaultPermissions(role: Role): readonly ("products.view" | "products.create" | "products.edit" | "products.price" | "products.archive" | "inventory.view" | "inventory.receive" | "inventory.adjust" | "inventory.count" | ... 33 more ... | "data.export")[];
```

Resolves the role's permissions plus grants minus revocations; unknown input fails closed. `base` is the shop's
edited set for a staff role (ADR-037); undefined or null means the built-in defaults. The owner always holds every
permission the role gives, whatever `base` says.

```ts
resolvePermissions(role: Role, granted: readonly Permission[], revoked: readonly Permission[], base?: readonly Permission[] | null): readonly ("products.view" | "products.create" | "products.edit" | "products.price" | "products.archive" | "inventory.view" | "inventory.receive" | "inventory.adjust" | "inventory.count" | ... 33 more ... | "data.export")[];
```

## [packages/contracts/src/sync.ts](../../packages/contracts/src/sync.ts)

Largest base64 ciphertext of one sealed fact: a 1 MiB signed event plus framing, base64-encoded.

```ts
const MAX_SEALED_CIPHERTEXT: 1500000;
```

HTTP sync API version served under `/api/v2`.

```ts
const SYNC_API_VERSION: 2;
```

Pull page size when the client does not ask for one, and the most a page may hold.

```ts
const PULL_DEFAULT_LIMIT: 100;
```

```ts
const PULL_MAX_LIMIT: 1000;
```

Public server identity; clients pin `public_key` only after the owner confirms `fingerprint`.

```ts
const serverIdentitySchema: ZodObject<{ api_version: ZodLiteral<2>; server_device_id: ZodUUID; public_key: ZodString; fingerprint: ZodString; enrollment_open: ZodBoolean; }, $strict>;
```

A single-use nonce the device signs to enroll its shop or open a network session.

```ts
const challengeRequestSchema: ZodObject<{ purpose: ZodEnum<{ enrollment: "enrollment"; session: "session"; }>; business_id: ZodUUID; device_id: ZodUUID; }, $strict>;
```

```ts
const challengeSchema: ZodObject<{ challenge_id: ZodUUID; nonce: ZodString; expires_at: ZodISODateTime; }, $strict>;
```

What an owner's authority-capable device signs to attach an existing local shop, keeping its IDs.

```ts
const enrollmentClaimSchema: ZodObject<{ format: ZodLiteral<"kolo.server-enrollment">; format_version: ZodLiteral<1>; challenge_id: ZodUUID; nonce: ZodString; server_device_id: ZodUUID; business: ZodObject<{ id: ZodUUID; name: ZodString; }, $strict>; owner_id: ZodUUID; device: ZodObject<{ id: ZodUUID; label: ZodString; platform: ZodEnum<{ ANDROID: "ANDROID"; IOS: "IOS"; DESKTOP: "DESKTOP"; }>; public_key: ZodString; }, $strict>; }, $strict>;
```

The single-use connection token travels beside the signed claim; the server stores only its hash. Re-attaching an already attached shop from its enrolling device needs none.

```ts
const enrollmentRequestSchema: ZodObject<{ claim: ZodObject<{ format: ZodLiteral<"kolo.server-enrollment">; format_version: ZodLiteral<1>; challenge_id: ZodUUID; nonce: ZodString; server_device_id: ZodUUID; business: ZodObject<{ id: ZodUUID; name: ZodString; }, $strict>; owner_id: ZodUUID; device: ZodObject<{ id: ZodUUID; label: ZodString; platform: ZodEnum<{ ANDROID: "ANDROID"; IOS: "IOS"; DESKTOP: "DESKTOP"; }>; public_key: ZodString; }, $strict>; }, $strict>; signature: ZodString; connection_code: ZodOptional<ZodString>; }, $strict>;
```

```ts
const sessionSchema: ZodObject<{ token: ZodString; expires_at: ZodISODateTime; business_id: ZodUUID; device_id: ZodUUID; server_device_id: ZodUUID; }, $strict>;
```

Facts the server stores and reads in the clear: device trust, device encryption keys, sealed shop-key grants
and the shop's server address. Every other fact crosses the server sealed with the shop key (ADR-030).

```ts
const CLEAR_EVENT_TYPES: readonly ["DEVICE_TRUSTED", "DEVICE_REVOKED", "DEVICE_ENCRYPTION_KEY", "SHOP_KEY_GRANTED", "SERVER_ENDPOINT_SET", "SERVER_ENDPOINT_CLEARED"];
```

True when a fact of this type may cross the server unsealed.

```ts
isClearEventType(type: string): boolean;
```

A signed fact encrypted with a shop key. The header is what the server sees: identity, origin position, the
digest of the inner signed fact (for duplicate detection) and which key sealed it. The header is the
AES-GCM additional data, so a changed header fails decryption on the till.

```ts
const sealedEventSchema: ZodObject<{ event_id: ZodUUID; origin_device_id: ZodUUID; origin_sequence: ZodNumber; digest: ZodString; key_id: ZodUUID; epoch: ZodNumber; nonce: ZodString; ciphertext: ZodString; tag: ZodString; }, $strict>;
```

```ts
const receiptResponseSchema: ZodObject<{ acknowledged: ZodNumber; }, $strict>;
```

```ts
const syncStatusSchema: ZodObject<{ pending: ZodNumber; delivered: ZodNumber; conflicts: ZodNumber; devices: ZodNumber; }, $strict>;
```

Hosted service eligibility for the session's shop (ADR-031). `unlimited` on self-hosted servers; `grace` after the
paid period while sync still runs; `inactive` when sync is paused. Never a condition for local trading.

```ts
const entitlementSchema: ZodObject<{ edition: ZodEnum<{ selfhost: "selfhost"; hosted: "hosted"; }>; status: ZodEnum<{ active: "active"; inactive: "inactive"; grace: "grace"; unlimited: "unlimited"; }>; paid_until: ZodNullable<ZodISODateTime>; grace_until: ZodNullable<ZodISODateTime>; }, $strict>;
```

The shop's till limit (ADR-039): `max_tills` null is unlimited; `tills` counts the active tills that hold a place.
A separate endpoint, since tills parse `entitlementSchema` strictly.

```ts
const planSchema: ZodObject<{ max_tills: ZodNullable<ZodNumber>; tills: ZodNumber; }, $strict>;
```

Stable failure body; never contains payloads, credentials or internals.

```ts
const syncErrorSchema: ZodObject<{ error: ZodObject<{ code: ZodString; retry: ZodOptional<ZodBoolean>; }, $strict>; }, $strict>;
```

Domain-separated UTF-8 bytes signed for enrollment.

```ts
enrollmentSigningMessage(claim: EnrollmentClaim): string;
```

Domain-separated UTF-8 bytes signed for a network session.

```ts
sessionSigningMessage(claim: SessionClaim): string;
```

Short key fingerprint shown on both the server console and the owner's device before pinning.

```ts
const KEY_FINGERPRINT_PREFIX: "kolo.pairing-key.v1\0";
```

Domain-separated UTF-8 bytes signed for a server sync batch.

```ts
syncBatchSigningMessage(batch: SyncBatch): string;
```

AES-GCM additional data binding a sealed fact's visible header to its shop.

```ts
sealedEventAad(businessId: string, sealed: Omit<SealedEvent, "nonce" | "ciphertext" | "tag">): string;
```

AES-GCM additional data binding a sealed shop key to its shop, key and recipient.

```ts
sealedKeyAad(businessId: string, keyId: string, epoch: number, deviceId: string): string;
```

Everything a till needs to reach a server: its address, pinned fingerprint and, for a first attachment, a single-use token.

```ts
export interface ConnectionCode {
    readonly url: string;
    readonly fingerprint: string;
    readonly token?: string;
}
```

One copyable string (also rendered as a QR code). The token is omitted when sharing the address with the shop's other tills.

```ts
formatConnectionCode(code: ConnectionCode): string;
```

Finds and checks a connection code inside pasted text. Returns undefined for anything malformed;
the address still has to pass the client's own URL policy.

```ts
parseConnectionCode(text: string): ConnectionCode;
```

Join codes (ADR-032): 10 Crockford base32 characters (50 bits) shown as `XXXX-XXXX-XX` on the owner's till. The
server only ever sees `joinIdOf(code)`; both tills derive it and a MAC key from an Argon2id key of the code (ADR-044).

```ts
const JOIN_CODE_ALPHABET: "0123456789ABCDEFGHJKMNPQRSTVWXYZ";
```

Normalizes typed input: case, spaces and dashes are ignored; O→0, I/L→1 as Crockford base32 reads them.

```ts
normalizeJoinCode(text: string): string;
```

`K7F2-9QXM-4D`.

```ts
formatJoinCode(code: string): string;
```

What the QR code carries: the server address and fingerprint, so the new till needs nothing else.

```ts
formatJoinLink(input: {
    readonly url: string;
    readonly fingerprint: string;
    readonly code: string;
}): string;
```

Reads a scanned or pasted join link.

```ts
parseJoinLink(text: string): { readonly url: string; readonly fingerprint: string; readonly code: string; };
```

A join mailbox as created by the owner: the signed invite, tagged with the code-derived key.

```ts
const joinOfferSchema: ZodObject<{ join_id: ZodString; invite: ZodObject<{ invite: ZodObject<{ format: ZodLiteral<"kolo.pairing-invite">; format_version: ZodLiteral<1>; invite_id: ZodUUID; business_id: ZodUUID; business_name: ZodString; owner_id: ZodUUID; owner_name: ZodString; source_device_id: ZodUUID; source_public_key: ZodString; created_at: ZodISODateTime; expires_at: ZodISODateTime; nonce: ZodString; authority_allowed: ZodBoolean; }, $strict>; digest: ZodString; signature: ZodString; }, $strict>; mac: ZodString; }, $strict>;
```

The new till's signed request, its X25519 key for the sealed password, both tagged with the code-derived key.

```ts
const joinRequestSchema: ZodObject<{ request: ZodObject<{ request: ZodObject<{ format: ZodLiteral<"kolo.pairing-request">; format_version: ZodLiteral<1>; request_id: ZodUUID; invite: ZodObject<{ invite: ZodObject<{ format: ZodLiteral<"kolo.pairing-invite">; format_version: ZodLiteral<1>; invite_id: ZodUUID; business_id: ZodUUID; business_name: ZodString; owner_id: ZodUUID; owner_name: ZodString; source_device_id: ZodUUID; source_public_key: ZodString; created_at: ZodISODateTime; expires_at: ZodISODateTime; nonce: ZodString; authority_allowed: ZodBoolean; }, $strict>; digest: ZodString; signature: ZodString; }, $strict>; installation_id: ZodUUID; device_id: ZodUUID; public_key: ZodString; label: ZodString; platform: ZodEnum<{ ANDROID: "ANDROID"; IOS: "IOS"; DESKTOP: "DESKTOP"; }>; created_at: ZodISODateTime; }, $strict>; digest: ZodString; signature: ZodString; }, $strict>; encryption_public_key: ZodString; mac: ZodString; }, $strict>;
```

Largest base64 enrollment a join mailbox (hosted server or a desktop's local listener) carries: about
36 MiB of encrypted container, a few months of a busy shop. A larger shop pairs by file.

```ts
const MAX_JOIN_ENROLLMENT_CHARS: number;
```

The owner's encrypted shop file (base64 of its container) and its password sealed to the new till's key.

```ts
const joinEnrollmentSchema: ZodObject<{ enrollment: ZodString; sealed_password: ZodObject<{ ephemeral_public_key: ZodString; nonce: ZodString; ciphertext: ZodString; tag: ZodString; }, $strict>; mac: ZodString; }, $strict>;
```

```ts
const joinStateSchema: ZodObject<{ state: ZodEnum<{ waiting: "waiting"; requested: "requested"; approved: "approved"; }>; expires_at: ZodISODateTime; }, $strict>;
```

```ts
export type JoinOffer = z.infer<typeof joinOfferSchema>;
```

```ts
export type JoinRequestBody = z.infer<typeof joinRequestSchema>;
```

```ts
export type JoinEnrollment = z.infer<typeof joinEnrollmentSchema>;
```

## [packages/contracts/src/transfer.ts](../../packages/contracts/src/transfer.ts)

```ts
const transferUuid: ZodUUID;
```

```ts
const transferTime: ZodISODateTime;
```

```ts
const transferDigest: ZodString;
```

```ts
const transferSignature: ZodString;
```

```ts
const transferKey: ZodString;
```

Size bounds a transfer reader or writer checks before key derivation or allocation.

```ts
export interface TransferLimits {
    readonly snapshotBytes: number;
    readonly contentBytes: number;
    readonly fileBytes: number;
}
```

The one size limit for backups and enrollment snapshots on every device (ADR-035): a 1 GiB SQLite
image. Files are whole byte arrays in memory, so peak memory is a few times the snapshot; users are
expected to have several GiB. The file bound adds 1 MiB to the content for deflate's stored-block
overhead on incompressible content (under 0.01%), chunk tags (16 bytes per MiB) and the header.

```ts
const TRANSFER_LIMITS: TransferLimits;
```

Largest raw SQLite image a backup or enrollment may carry.

```ts
const MAX_SNAPSHOT_IMAGE_BYTES: number;
```

Largest decompressed transfer content.

```ts
const MAX_TRANSFER_CONTENT_BYTES: number;
```

Largest encrypted transfer file on disk.

```ts
const MAX_ENCRYPTED_FILE_BYTES: number;
```

Purposes an encrypted transfer file may declare.

```ts
const transferPurposeSchema: ZodEnum<{ backup: "backup"; enrollment: "enrollment"; receipt: "receipt"; exchange: "exchange"; }>;
```

Purposes an encrypted transfer file may declare.

```ts
export type TransferPurpose = z.infer<typeof transferPurposeSchema>;
```

First eight bytes of an encrypted container (ADR-035): `\x89KOLO\r\n\x1a`.

```ts
const CONTAINER_MAGIC: readonly number[];
```

Magic plus the four-byte big-endian header length.

```ts
const CONTAINER_PREFIX_BYTES: 12;
```

Largest canonical content header a container may declare.

```ts
const MAX_CONTAINER_HEADER_BYTES: 4096;
```

Largest canonical key-slot section a container may declare.

```ts
const MAX_CONTAINER_SLOTS_BYTES: 16384;
```

Most key slots one container may carry.

```ts
const MAX_KEY_SLOTS: 8;
```

AES-GCM tag stored after every chunk.

```ts
const CONTAINER_TAG_BYTES: 16;
```

Chunk size writers use; readers accept any power of two from 4 KiB to 4 MiB.

```ts
const CONTAINER_CHUNK_BYTES: number;
```

The HPKE suite of recipient slots: base mode, DHKEM(X25519, HKDF-SHA256), HKDF-SHA256, AES-256-GCM (RFC 9180).

```ts
const RECIPIENT_SUITE: "HPKE-base-X25519-SHA256-AES256GCM";
```

Immutable content header, format version 3 (ADR-035). It is the canonical JSON after the 12-byte
prefix; magic, length and header together are the additional data of every chunk. `recipients`
lists the recovery keys sealed when the file was written, so a removed slot is detectable.

```ts
const containerHeaderSchema: ZodObject<{ format: ZodLiteral<"kolo.encrypted">; format_version: ZodLiteral<3>; purpose: ZodEnum<{ backup: "backup"; enrollment: "enrollment"; receipt: "receipt"; exchange: "exchange"; }>; file_id: ZodUUID; cipher: ZodLiteral<"AES-256-GCM">; compression: ZodLiteral<"deflate">; nonce_prefix: ZodString; chunk_bytes: ZodNumber; content_bytes: ZodNumber; recipients: ZodArray<ZodString>; }, $strict>;
```

Parsed container header.

```ts
export type ContainerHeader = z.infer<typeof containerHeaderSchema>;
```

A password slot: Argon2id over the NFC-normalized UTF-8 password wraps the file key (bounded parameters).

```ts
const passwordSlotSchema: ZodObject<{ type: ZodLiteral<"password">; kdf: ZodLiteral<"argon2id">; memory_kib: ZodNumber; passes: ZodNumber; parallelism: ZodNumber; salt: ZodString; nonce: ZodString; wrapped: ZodString; }, $strict>;
```

A recipient slot: the file key sealed with single-shot HPKE to a shop backup recovery key (ADR-036).

```ts
const recipientSlotSchema: ZodObject<{ type: ZodLiteral<"recipient">; suite: ZodLiteral<"HPKE-base-X25519-SHA256-AES256GCM">; key_id: ZodString; enc: ZodString; wrapped: ZodString; }, $strict>;
```

The mutable key-slot section: at most one password slot, first, then recipient slots in ascending
`key_id` order, so each set of slots has one encoding.

```ts
const keySlotsSchema: ZodObject<{ slots: ZodArray<ZodDiscriminatedUnion<[ZodObject<{ type: ZodLiteral<"password">; kdf: ZodLiteral<"argon2id">; memory_kib: ZodNumber; passes: ZodNumber; parallelism: ZodNumber; salt: ZodString; nonce: ZodString; wrapped: ZodString; }, $strict>, ZodObject<{ type: ZodLiteral<"recipient">; suite: ZodLiteral<"HPKE-base-X25519-SHA256-AES256GCM">; key_id: ZodString; enc: ZodString; wrapped: ZodString; }, $strict>], "type">>; }, $strict>;
```

Parsed key slots.

```ts
export type KeySlots = z.infer<typeof keySlotsSchema>;
```

Snapshot boundary includes origin coverage and permanent application count, never a global server cursor.

```ts
const snapshotBoundarySchema: ZodObject<{ audit_watermark: ZodNumber; applied_count: ZodNumber; origins: ZodArray<ZodObject<{ device_id: ZodUUID; maximum_sequence: ZodNumber; next_local_sequence: ZodNullable<ZodNumber>; }, $strict>>; legacy: ZodArray<ZodObject<{ business_id: ZodUUID; provenance: ZodLiteral<"unsigned_v1_snapshot">; outbox_next: ZodNumber; audit_watermark: ZodNumber; }, $strict>>; }, $strict>;
```

Signed checksum/identity manifest for a sanitized physical SQLite snapshot.

```ts
const snapshotManifestSchema: ZodObject<{ format: ZodLiteral<"kolo.snapshot">; format_version: ZodLiteral<1>; snapshot_id: ZodUUID; kind: ZodEnum<{ backup: "backup"; enrollment: "enrollment"; }>; schema_version: ZodNumber; created_at: ZodISODateTime; business: ZodObject<{ id: ZodUUID; name: ZodString; currency: ZodLiteral<"XAF">; }, $strict>; owner: ZodObject<{ id: ZodUUID; name: ZodString; }, $strict>; source_device_id: ZodUUID; source_public_key: ZodString; snapshot_digest: ZodString; snapshot_bytes: ZodNumber; boundary: ZodObject<{ audit_watermark: ZodNumber; applied_count: ZodNumber; origins: ZodArray<ZodObject<{ device_id: ZodUUID; maximum_sequence: ZodNumber; next_local_sequence: ZodNullable<ZodNumber>; }, $strict>>; legacy: ZodArray<ZodObject<{ business_id: ZodUUID; provenance: ZodLiteral<"unsigned_v1_snapshot">; outbox_next: ZodNumber; audit_watermark: ZodNumber; }, $strict>>; }, $strict>; credential_policy: ZodLiteral<"device_local_credentials_excluded">; target: ZodNullable<ZodObject<{ installation_id: ZodUUID; device_id: ZodUUID; public_key: ZodString; label: ZodString; platform: ZodEnum<{ ANDROID: "ANDROID"; IOS: "IOS"; DESKTOP: "DESKTOP"; }>; authority_allowed: ZodBoolean; invite_id: ZodUUID; request_id: ZodUUID; request_digest: ZodString; }, $strict>>; shop_keys: ZodOptional<ZodArray<ZodObject<{ key_id: ZodUUID; epoch: ZodNumber; key: ZodString; }, $strict>>>; }, $strict>;
```

```ts
const signedSnapshotSchema: ZodObject<{ manifest: ZodObject<{ format: ZodLiteral<"kolo.snapshot">; format_version: ZodLiteral<1>; snapshot_id: ZodUUID; kind: ZodEnum<{ backup: "backup"; enrollment: "enrollment"; }>; schema_version: ZodNumber; created_at: ZodISODateTime; business: ZodObject<{ id: ZodUUID; name: ZodString; currency: ZodLiteral<"XAF">; }, $strict>; owner: ZodObject<{ id: ZodUUID; name: ZodString; }, $strict>; source_device_id: ZodUUID; source_public_key: ZodString; snapshot_digest: ZodString; snapshot_bytes: ZodNumber; boundary: ZodObject<{ audit_watermark: ZodNumber; applied_count: ZodNumber; origins: ZodArray<ZodObject<{ device_id: ZodUUID; maximum_sequence: ZodNumber; next_local_sequence: ZodNullable<ZodNumber>; }, $strict>>; legacy: ZodArray<ZodObject<{ business_id: ZodUUID; provenance: ZodLiteral<"unsigned_v1_snapshot">; outbox_next: ZodNumber; audit_watermark: ZodNumber; }, $strict>>; }, $strict>; credential_policy: ZodLiteral<"device_local_credentials_excluded">; target: ZodNullable<ZodObject<{ installation_id: ZodUUID; device_id: ZodUUID; public_key: ZodString; label: ZodString; platform: ZodEnum<{ ANDROID: "ANDROID"; IOS: "IOS"; DESKTOP: "DESKTOP"; }>; authority_allowed: ZodBoolean; invite_id: ZodUUID; request_id: ZodUUID; request_digest: ZodString; }, $strict>>; shop_keys: ZodOptional<ZodArray<ZodObject<{ key_id: ZodUUID; epoch: ZodNumber; key: ZodString; }, $strict>>>; }, $strict>; digest: ZodString; signature: ZodString; }, $strict>;
```

