## [packages/core/src/application/audit.ts](../../packages/core/src/application/audit.ts)

The operation journal (spec §39, `audit.view`): who did what, when, on which device.

Read from the signed event journal, which holds every till's facts once they have synced, plus the few checks that
only this device records because they change nothing (a cash count, a correction or stock count that matched).
Nothing is stored for the view; entries are summarized on demand from the immutable facts. Clients localize from
`type`, `subject` and `facts`; no display text is produced here, and no key material leaves a payload.

Families the journal can be narrowed to.

```ts
export type AuditGroup = "sales" | "purchases" | "stock" | "money" | "catalogue" | "people" | "staff" | "devices" | "settings" | "other";
```

```ts
const AUDIT_GROUPS: readonly AuditGroup[];
```

The family an event or local check belongs to; names this version does not know are `other`.

```ts
auditGroupOf(type: string): AuditGroup;
```

What an entry is about, or what it involved besides. Names are as recorded at the time when the fact carries one.

```ts
export interface AuditSubject {
    readonly kind: "product" | "category" | "customer" | "supplier" | "account" | "member" | "device" | "sale" | "purchase" | "shop";
    readonly id: string;
    readonly name?: string | undefined;
}
```

Figures and codes for one entry; each type fills the ones that apply. Money is XAF, quantity thousandths.

```ts
export interface AuditFacts {
    readonly amountMinor?: number;
    readonly previousAmountMinor?: number;
    readonly costMinor?: number;
    readonly previousCostMinor?: number;
    readonly quantityThousandths?: number;
    readonly lines?: number;
    readonly code?: string;
    readonly reason?: string;
    readonly reference?: string;
    readonly created?: boolean;
    readonly active?: boolean;
    readonly reversal?: boolean;
    readonly expected?: number;
    readonly counted?: number;
    readonly days?: number | null;
    readonly rateBasisPoints?: number;
    readonly enabled?: boolean;
}
```

One journal entry, newest first.

```ts
export interface AuditEntry {
    readonly id: string;
    readonly type: string;
    readonly group: AuditGroup;
    readonly occurredAt: string;
    readonly actor: {
        readonly id: string;
        readonly name?: string | undefined;
    };
    readonly device: {
        readonly id: string;
        readonly label?: string | undefined;
    };
    readonly source: "journal" | "this_device";
    readonly status?: string | undefined;
    readonly subject?: AuditSubject | undefined;
    readonly related?: AuditSubject | undefined;
    readonly facts: AuditFacts;
}
```

Narrowing for . Times are a half-open ISO range; IDs come from earlier entries or the lists returned.

```ts
export interface AuditQuery {
    readonly from?: string | undefined;
    readonly until?: string | undefined;
    readonly actorId?: string | undefined;
    readonly deviceId?: string | undefined;
    readonly group?: AuditGroup | undefined;
    readonly subjectId?: string | undefined;
    readonly after?: Cursor | undefined;
    readonly limit?: number | undefined;
}
```

A page of the journal with the shop's members and devices, for naming filters.

```ts
export interface AuditPage {
    readonly items: readonly AuditEntry[];
    readonly next?: Cursor | undefined;
    readonly members: readonly {
        readonly id: string;
        readonly name: string;
    }[];
    readonly devices: readonly {
        readonly id: string;
        readonly label: string;
    }[];
}
```

One page of the operation journal under `audit.view`, newest first, narrowed by period, member, device, family or
record. Received facts appear once synced, with the member and device that made them; a held-back fact shows its status.

```ts
auditTrail(context: OperationContext, query: AuditQuery = {}): AuditPage;
```

Every entry matching `query` (its cursor and limit ignored), newest first, read page by page up to
; `truncated` says more matched. Taking the journal away needs `data.export` as well as
`audit.view`, checked on every page, so a revocation stops an export already under way.

```ts
auditExport(context: OperationContext, query: Omit<AuditQuery, "after" | "limit"> = {}, maximum = AUDIT_EXPORT_LIMIT): { readonly items: readonly AuditEntry[]; readonly truncated: boolean; };
```

## [packages/core/src/application/backup-key.ts](../../packages/core/src/application/backup-key.ts)

A backup recovery key shown to the owner once (ADR-036) and not yet pinned. `text` is the only copy of the secret;
`confirm` lists the three 0-based group indices the owner must type back before 
pins the public half. Discarding the object discards the key.

```ts
export interface PendingBackupKey {
    readonly text: string;
    readonly keyId: string;
    readonly confirm: readonly number[];
}
```

A pinned backup recovery key as the shop sees it: its ID, creation date and whether new backups seal to it.

```ts
export interface BackupKeyView {
    readonly keyId: string;
    readonly version: number;
    readonly createdAt: string;
    readonly current: boolean;
}
```

Creates a backup recovery key after a fresh owner PIN proof and returns it for showing once. Nothing is stored:
the secret lives only in the returned text, and the public half is pinned only after confirmation.

```ts
prepareBackupRecoveryKey(context: OperationContext, secret: string, crypto: TransferCrypto): Promise<PendingBackupKey>;
```

Pins a prepared key once the owner typed back the requested groups: an owner-signed `SHOP_BACKUP_KEY` fact with the
next version. New backups then seal to it; earlier keys stay pinned for the backups that need them.

```ts
confirmBackupRecoveryKey(context: OperationContext, key: PendingBackupKey, typed: readonly string[], crypto: TransferCrypto): Promise<BackupKeyView>;
```

The shop's pinned backup recovery keys, newest first; the first is the one new backups seal to.

```ts
backupRecoveryKeys(context: OperationContext): readonly BackupKeyView[];
```

The recipient new backups seal to: the current pinned key, read from this till's own database (never from a file or the
network), with its ID checked against its public key so a corrupted row can never redirect encryption.

```ts
currentBackupRecipient(context: OperationContext, crypto: Pick<TransferCrypto, "sha256Bytes">): Promise<RecoveryRecipient>;
```

## [packages/core/src/application/backup.ts](../../packages/core/src/application/backup.ts)

Verified snapshot summary; the native candidate is held privately and consumed at most once.

```ts
export interface BackupPreview {
    readonly businessId: string;
    readonly businessName: string;
    readonly ownerName: string;
    readonly createdAt: string;
    readonly sourceDeviceId: string;
    readonly snapshotId: string;
    readonly schemaVersion: number;
    readonly appliedEvents: number;
}
```

Backup choices. Photos are included unless `includePhotos` is false; a backup without them restores products with no photo.

```ts
export interface BackupOptions {
    readonly includePhotos?: boolean;
}
```

Streams a consistent encrypted backup into `sink` (a private temporary file on a till), chunk by chunk, so memory stays a
few MiB whatever the shop size (ADR-035). It excludes credentials, cloud sessions and installation private keys, and
optionally product photos. It opens with `password` and, once the owner pinned one, with the shop's backup recovery key;
`password` null makes a recovery-key-only backup (automatic backups), which needs a pinned key. It carries the shop data
keys this till holds, so a restore can resume sync with no other till (ADR-036); when a granted key cannot be opened
(a vault failure) it writes nothing and fails with `sync_keys_unavailable`. Returns the file name to offer.

```ts
writeBackup(context: OperationContext, password: string | null, files: SnapshotPort, crypto: TransferCrypto, sink: ByteSink, options: BackupOptions = {}): Promise<{ readonly filename: string; }>;
```

 into memory, for callers that hand the whole file to a dialog or a test.

```ts
createBackup(context: OperationContext, password: string | null, files: SnapshotPort, crypto: TransferCrypto, options: BackupOptions = {}): Promise<TransferDocument>;
```

How a backup is opened: its password, or the shop's backup recovery key as the owner typed it (ADR-036).

```ts
export type BackupSecret = string | {
    readonly recoveryKey: string;
};
```

Opens a backup from a reader (a selected file read in chunks), with its password or typed recovery key. The image is
decrypted and decompressed straight into a private staging file, never whole in memory, then the slot, image digest
and signed manifest are authenticated and the database is validated (schema, integrity, scope, foreign keys, replay
boundary) in isolation. Anything that fails discards the staging file.

```ts
previewBackupFile(reader: ByteReader, secret: BackupSecret, files: SnapshotPort, crypto: TransferCrypto): Promise<BackupPreview>;
```

 for a backup already in memory.

```ts
previewBackup(file: Uint8Array, secret: BackupSecret, files: SnapshotPort, crypto: TransferCrypto): Promise<BackupPreview>;
```

Releases an unused private candidate; cannot remove an activated business database.

```ts
cancelBackupPreview(preview: BackupPreview): Promise<void>;
```

Explicit owner-confirmed replacement: fresh native identity, preserved history, atomic active-slot switch and old file retained.

```ts
restoreWorkspace(preview: BackupPreview, input: {
    readonly confirmedBusinessId: string;
    readonly deviceLabel: string;
    readonly platform: "ANDROID" | "IOS" | "DESKTOP";
    readonly secret: string;
    readonly recoveryContext?: OperationContext;
}, ports: WorkspacePorts): Promise<{ database: Database; installation: Installation; }>;
```

## [packages/core/src/application/event-application.ts](../../packages/core/src/application/event-application.ts)

Stores an origin-verified fact durably, then applies effects and the permanent identity together.

```ts
applyEvent(context: OperationContext, token: VerifiedEvent): EventOutcome;
```

Bounded dependency passes; no network, re-signing or rewriting of applied identities.

```ts
retryDeferred(context: OperationContext, limit = 200, maxPasses = 4): number;
```

Reasons an owner may approve a held-back fact for; anything else (malformed, forged, conflicting identity) stays refused.

```ts
const REVIEWABLE_REASONS: readonly ["permission_denied", "authority_revision", "device_revoked", "return_conflict"];
```

A fact from another till held back for a reason the owner may approve, as the review list shows it.

```ts
export interface HeldBackFact {
    readonly eventId: string;
    readonly digest: string;
    readonly type: string;
    readonly reason: (typeof REVIEWABLE_REASONS)[number];
    readonly deviceId: string;
    readonly deviceLabel: string | null;
    readonly actorName: string | null;
    readonly occurredAt: string;
}
```

Owner only: the facts waiting for approval, oldest first (at most 200). Authority changes are never listed.

```ts
heldBackFacts(context: OperationContext): readonly HeldBackFact[];
```

Owner explicitly approves the exact signed business fact held back for stale/revoked authority, a revoked device's
late history (ADR-040) or a return that raced another (ADR-041). Each approval covers only its reason, so a fact may
need another one for the next check; every other strict check still applies and malformed data still fails.

```ts
reviewQuarantinedEvent(context: OperationContext, eventId: string, digest: string): EventOutcome;
```

## [packages/core/src/application/event-delivery.ts](../../packages/core/src/application/event-delivery.ts)

Pending signed facts for exactly one destination, preserving their original immutable envelope/proof. `types` limits the event types returned.

```ts
eventsForDestination(context: OperationContext, destinationId: string, limit = 200, types?: readonly string[]): readonly { envelope: { event_id: string; business_id: string; origin_device_id: string; actor_id: string; origin_sequence: number; event_type: string; event_version: number; occurred_at: string; authority_revision: number; required_permissions: ("products.view" | ... 41 more ... | "data.export")[]; payload: Record<....;
```

Counts scoped delivery/application work without changing HTTP v1's independent cloud queue.

```ts
exchangeStatus(context: OperationContext, destinationId: string): Readonly<Record<string, number>>;
```

Bounded signed forwarding batch; offering never acknowledges delivery. Empty queues return undefined.

```ts
createJournalBatch(context: OperationContext, destinationId: string, crypto: ExchangeCrypto, limit = 200): Promise<{ batch: { format: "kolo.events"; format_version: 2; batch_id: string; business_id: string; sender_device_id: string; receiver_device_id: string; created_at: string; events: { envelope: { event_id: string; ... 10 more ...; branch_id?: string; }; digest: string; signature: string; }[]; }; digest: string; sign...;
```

Read-only authentication of the container and every origin it can currently verify; no receipt or application
state is written. Events whose origin is not yet trusted here are returned separately: trust may arrive in the
same batch (an owner-signed `DEVICE_TRUSTED`), and they are never stored or acknowledged until verified.

```ts
verifyJournalBatch(context: OperationContext, input: unknown, crypto: ExchangeCrypto): Promise<{ batch: { format: "kolo.events"; format_version: 2; batch_id: string; business_id: string; sender_device_id: string; receiver_device_id: string; created_at: string; events: { envelope: { event_id: string; ... 10 more ...; branch_id?: string; }; digest: string; signature: string; }[]; }; digest: string; sign...;
```

One imported event's outcome; `untrusted` means its origin was not trusted here and nothing was stored, and
`locked` that it arrived sealed with a shop key this device has not been granted yet. Neither is acknowledged.

```ts
export type ImportOutcome = EventOutcome | "untrusted" | "locked";
```

Authenticates the forwarding sender and every preserved origin before any import work is stored.

```ts
importJournalBatch(context: OperationContext, input: unknown, crypto: ExchangeCrypto): Promise<readonly { eventId: string; outcome: ImportOutcome; }[]>;
```

Signs only exact durable, non-quarantined imports; this can be regenerated after a lost receipt.

```ts
createPeerReceipt(context: OperationContext, batchId: string, crypto: ExchangeCrypto): Promise<{ receipt: { format: "kolo.receipt"; format_version: 1; receipt_id: string; business_id: string; sender_device_id: string; receiver_device_id: string; batch_id: string; created_at: string; entries: { ...; }[]; }; digest: string; signature: string; }>;
```

Authenticated, exact batch-bound acknowledgment updates this destination only; duplicates are harmless.

```ts
acknowledgeDelivery(context: OperationContext, destinationId: string, input: unknown, crypto: ExchangeCrypto): Promise<number>;
```

## [packages/core/src/application/exchange.ts](../../packages/core/src/application/exchange.ts)

The immutable domain-prefixed UTF-8 bytes hashed and signed for an event.

```ts
eventSigningMessage(event: SignedJournalEvent["envelope"]): string;
```

Completes signatures for durable local facts. Native crypto runs outside SQL;
signature attachment is immutable and recoverable. Lock during signing commits
no proof, and the pending fact remains. Historical v1 rows are never signed here.

```ts
sealPendingEvents(context: OperationContext, crypto: ExchangeCrypto, limit = 100): Promise<number>;
```

Verifies exact bounded content under a pinned origin and returns an opaque context-bound capability.

```ts
verifyEvent(context: OperationContext, input: unknown, crypto: ExchangeCrypto): Promise<VerifiedEvent>;
```

Owner-approved peer/server identity; F3 pairing must confirm keys before invoking this operation.

```ts
registerExchangeDestination(context: OperationContext, input: {
    readonly deviceId: string;
    readonly publicKey: string;
    readonly label: string;
    readonly platform: "ANDROID" | "IOS" | "DESKTOP" | "SERVER";
    readonly kind: "peer" | "server";
    readonly authorityAllowed?: boolean;
}): string;
```

Owner revokes/reinstates trust for another device; imported history stays immutable.

```ts
setExchangeDeviceActive(context: OperationContext, deviceId: string, active: boolean): void;
```

Publishes owner-signed trust for keys this authority device approved before trust propagated
(for example through earlier pairing), so devices that never met can verify each other's facts.
New signed facts are created; nothing historical is re-signed. Returns how many were published.

```ts
publishDeviceRoster(context: OperationContext): number;
```

## [packages/core/src/application/file-exchange.ts](../../packages/core/src/application/file-exchange.ts)

Read-only import summary. Authority/dependencies are checked again at application time.

```ts
export interface BundlePreview {
    readonly batchId: string;
    readonly businessId: string;
    readonly senderDeviceId: string;
    readonly events: readonly {
        readonly eventId: string;
        readonly type: string;
        readonly assessment: "ready" | "duplicate" | "deferred" | "review" | "unsupported";
    }[];
}
```

Encrypts one bounded signed batch; exported facts are offered until a receiver receipt is imported.

```ts
exportBundle(context: OperationContext, destinationId: string, password: string, crypto: TransferCrypto, limit = 200): Promise<TransferDocument>;
```

Decrypts bounded bytes and authenticates shop, destination, forwarder and all origins without applying facts.

```ts
previewBundle(context: OperationContext, file: Uint8Array, password: string, crypto: TransferCrypto): Promise<BundlePreview>;
```

Imports only an opaque authenticated preview and rechecks current trust/authority before any effects.

```ts
importBundle(context: OperationContext, preview: BundlePreview, crypto: TransferCrypto): Promise<readonly { eventId: string; outcome: ImportOutcome; }[]>;
```

Regenerates an encrypted durable receipt, including after a lost/cancelled file save.

```ts
exportReceipt(context: OperationContext, batchId: string, password: string, crypto: TransferCrypto): Promise<TransferDocument>;
```

Decrypts and applies exact digest acknowledgments to the expected destination only.

```ts
importReceipt(context: OperationContext, destinationId: string, file: Uint8Array, password: string, crypto: TransferCrypto): Promise<number>;
```

Known peer selection resolved from current SQLite state.

```ts
exchangeDestinations(context: OperationContext): readonly { id: string; label: string; deviceId: string; }[];
```

Durable imports available for receipt regeneration, newest first.

```ts
receivedBatches(context: OperationContext): readonly { batchId: string; senderDeviceId: string; createdAt: string; }[];
```

Localized diagnostics identify the exact fact and an actionable recovery path, without exposing financial payloads.

```ts
exchangeDiagnostics(context: OperationContext, locale: "fr" | "en" = "fr"): readonly { eventId: string; code: string; description: string; action: string; }[];
```

## [packages/core/src/application/http-sync-transport.ts](../../packages/core/src/application/http-sync-transport.ts)

Minimal `fetch` shape, so mobile, desktop and tests can each supply their platform implementation.

```ts
export type FetchPort = (url: string, init: {
    method: string;
    headers: Record<string, string>;
    body?: string;
    signal: AbortSignal;
    redirect?: "error";
}) => Promise<{
    readonly status: number;
    text(): Promise<string>;
}>;
```

HTTP implementation of the sync transport. Responses are bounded and validated against the shared
contracts before use; the server's error codes are passed through, never its bodies or internals.

```ts
createHttpSyncTransport(baseUrl: string, options: {
    readonly fetch?: FetchPort;
    readonly timeoutMs?: number;
} = {}): SyncTransport;
```

## [packages/core/src/application/join.ts](../../packages/core/src/application/join.ts)

Join by code (ADR-032): the existing pairing (signed invite, signed request, owner confirmation, encrypted shop
snapshot) carried through a short-lived server mailbox instead of files. A 10-character code shown on the owner's till
replaces the fingerprint and code comparisons: both tills tag their messages with a key derived from it, so a server
that swapped either message is detected. The shop file's password is random and sealed to the new till's own key, so
neither the server nor anyone who later learns the code can open it. The owner still approves the named till.

A join the owner's till has opened. `token` is a network session kept only in memory while it waits.

```ts
export interface JoinStart {
    readonly code: string;
    readonly display: string;
    readonly link: string;
    readonly joinId: string;
    readonly expiresAt: string;
    readonly token: string;
    readonly inviteId: string;
    readonly authorityAllowed: boolean;
}
```

The till asking to join, verified against the code; shown to the owner before approval.

```ts
export interface JoinCandidate {
    readonly label: string;
    readonly platform: string;
    readonly requestText: string;
    readonly encryptionPublicKey: string;
}
```

What the new till learns from a code before choosing its own code: whose shop it is joining.

```ts
export interface JoinInvitation {
    readonly businessName: string;
    readonly ownerName: string;
    readonly authorityAllowed: boolean;
    readonly inviteText: string;
}
```

The server's handle for a code: derived from the code's Argon2id key, so the mailbox never names the code itself.

```ts
joinIdOf(code: string, crypto: SealingCrypto): Promise<string>;
```

Creates the authenticated join offer without choosing a transport; also used by a desktop's local mailbox.

```ts
prepareJoinOffer(context: OperationContext, crypto: SealingCrypto, input: {
    readonly url: string;
    readonly fingerprint: string;
    readonly authorityAllowed: boolean;
    readonly expiresAt: string;
}): Promise<{ start: JoinStart; offer: { join_id: string; invite: { invite: { format: "kolo.pairing-invite"; format_version: 1; invite_id: string; business_id: string; business_name: string; owner_id: string; ... 6 more ...; authority_allowed: boolean; }; digest: string; signature: string; }; mac: string; }; }>;
```

Verifies that a candidate holds the QR's secret and requests this exact invite, regardless of mailbox transport.

```ts
inspectJoinCandidate(body: JoinRequestBody, crypto: SealingCrypto, start: JoinStart): Promise<JoinCandidate>;
```

Builds the approved snapshot and seals its random password to the candidate; the mailbox never receives its plaintext.

```ts
prepareJoinEnrollment(context: OperationContext, crypto: SealingCrypto, files: SnapshotPort, start: JoinStart, candidate: JoinCandidate): Promise<{ enrollment: string; sealed_password: { ephemeral_public_key: string; nonce: string; ciphertext: string; tag: string; }; mac: string; }>;
```

Owner side: creates an invite for one till, opens its mailbox on the connected server and returns the code to show
(and the link for the QR code, which also carries the server's address and fingerprint).

```ts
startJoin(context: OperationContext, transport: SyncTransport, crypto: SealingCrypto, input: {
    readonly serverDeviceId: string;
    readonly url: string;
    readonly fingerprint: string;
    readonly authorityAllowed: boolean;
}): Promise<JoinStart>;
```

Owner side: the till that asked to join, once one has, after checking it holds the code and answers this invite.

```ts
pollJoin(transport: SyncTransport, crypto: SealingCrypto, start: JoinStart): Promise<JoinCandidate>;
```

Owner side: approves the named till. Pins its key exactly like file pairing, builds the encrypted shop file with a
random password, seals that password to the till's own key and posts both.

```ts
approveJoin(context: OperationContext, transport: SyncTransport, crypto: SealingCrypto, files: SnapshotPort, start: JoinStart, candidate: JoinCandidate): Promise<void>;
```

Owner side: refuses or abandons a join; the invite can no longer be used.

```ts
endJoin(context: OperationContext, transport: SyncTransport, start: JoinStart, refused: boolean): Promise<void>;
```

New till: reads whose shop a code opens. Refuses an invite not tagged with this exact code.

```ts
lookupJoin(transport: SyncTransport, crypto: SealingCrypto, code: string): Promise<JoinInvitation>;
```

New till: provisions this device's keys with its own new code, then posts its signed request and encryption key,
tagged with the join code. Safe to repeat after an interruption: the same request is posted again.

```ts
requestJoin(code: string, invitation: JoinInvitation, input: {
    readonly label: string;
    readonly platform: "ANDROID" | "IOS" | "DESKTOP";
    readonly secret: string;
}, ports: WorkspacePorts, transport: SyncTransport, crypto: SealingCrypto): Promise<void>;
```

New till: once the owner approved, opens the shop file with the sealed password and installs the shop exactly as
file pairing does. Returns undefined while the owner has not answered.

```ts
finishJoin(code: string, ports: WorkspacePorts, files: SnapshotPort, transport: SyncTransport, crypto: SealingCrypto): Promise<{ readonly database: Database; readonly installation: Installation; }>;
```

True while this empty device has a join request waiting for the owner, so the app can resume waiting after a restart.

```ts
joinPending(database: Database): boolean;
```

## [packages/core/src/application/local-join.ts](../../packages/core/src/application/local-join.ts)

LAN-only mailbox HTTP transport. Redirects are refused so a private QR cannot send a request to a public endpoint.

```ts
localJoinTransport(url: string): SyncTransport;
```

A verified invitation from a local desktop; the owner identity is pinned by the QR and code authentication.

```ts
lookupLocalJoin(link: string, transport: SyncTransport, crypto: SealingCrypto): Promise<JoinInvitation>;
```

Posts this installation's signed local join request using the existing recipient-bound pairing protocol.

```ts
requestLocalJoin(link: string, invitation: JoinInvitation, input: {
    label: string;
    platform: "DESKTOP" | "ANDROID" | "IOS";
    secret: string;
}, ports: WorkspacePorts, transport: SyncTransport, crypto: SealingCrypto): Promise<void>;
```

Finishes owner-approved local enrollment and stores only this device's host address, after validating the signed snapshot.

```ts
finishLocalJoin(link: string, ports: WorkspacePorts, files: SnapshotPort, transport: SyncTransport, crypto: SealingCrypto): Promise<{ readonly database: Database; readonly installation: Installation; }>;
```

## [packages/core/src/application/local-sync.ts](../../packages/core/src/application/local-sync.ts)

Encrypts to an approved device's X25519 key and signs the complete header/ciphertext with the native identity.

```ts
sealLocalPacket(context: OperationContext, peerId: string, recipientKey: string, payload: LocalExchangePayload, crypto: SealingCrypto, replyTo: string | null = null): Promise<{ header: { format: "kolo.local-sync"; version: 1; id: string; business_id: string; sender_device_id: string; receiver_device_id: string; encryption_public_key: string; ephemeral_public_key: string; nonce: string; reply_to?: string; }; ciphertext: string; tag: string; signature: string; }>;
```

Verifies current shop/device trust before decryption; private keys never leave the vault.

```ts
openLocalPacket(context: OperationContext, input: unknown, crypto: SealingCrypto, replyTo: string | null = null): Promise<{ packet: { header: { format: "kolo.local-sync"; version: 1; id: string; business_id: string; sender_device_id: string; receiver_device_id: string; encryption_public_key: string; ephemeral_public_key: string; nonce: string; reply_to?: string; }; ciphertext: string; tag: string; signature: string; }; payload:...;
```

Desktop-side direct exchange: apply authenticated facts/receipts and return an encrypted signed response.

```ts
answerLocalExchange(context: OperationContext, input: unknown, crypto: SealingCrypto): Promise<{ header: { format: "kolo.local-sync"; version: 1; id: string; business_id: string; sender_device_id: string; receiver_device_id: string; encryption_public_key: string; ephemeral_public_key: string; nonce: string; reply_to?: string; }; ciphertext: string; tag: string; signature: string; }>;
```

Connects a paired device to a literal LAN address. This device-local setting never becomes a signed business fact.

```ts
connectLocalPeer(context: OperationContext, peerId: string, url: string): void;
```

A known local host and the last delivery outcome, scoped to the unlocked shop.

```ts
export interface LocalPeerConnection {
    readonly deviceId: string;
    readonly label: string;
    readonly url: string | null;
    readonly lastSuccessAt: string | null;
    readonly lastError: string | null;
}
```

Paired local hosts configured on this device only.

```ts
localPeerConnections(context: OperationContext): readonly LocalPeerConnection[];
```

Client-side bidirectional sync with retry-safe journal facts and durable receiver receipts, over a local HTTP connection.

```ts
synchronizeLocalPeer(context: OperationContext, peerId: string, crypto: SealingCrypto, fetcher: FetchPort = (url, init) => fetch(url, init)): Promise<{ sent: number; received: number; }>;
```

Foreground schedule shared by desktop and mobile; individual host failures never prevent another host or local sales.

```ts
class LocalSyncScheduler {
  constructor(crypto: SealingCrypto, fetcher?: FetchPort, now = () => Date.now());
  /** Brings the next exchange forward after a local change or return to the foreground. */
  soon(): void;
  /** Runs at most one pass at a time, only while a valid signed-in context is available. */
  tick(context: OperationContext | undefined): Promise<void>;
}
```

## [packages/core/src/application/notifications.ts](../../packages/core/src/application/notifications.ts)

Local notifications (ADR-033, spec §49).

Derived on demand from this device's SQLite: nothing is stored about a condition except this user's read and
announced marks, keyed by a stable key and the fingerprint of the facts that made it. A changed fingerprint makes a
notification unread (and unannounced) again; a condition that clears simply disappears. Each kind is included only
for actors holding its permission, so a cashier is not told about supplier debts. Clients localize from `kind` and
`facts`; no display text is produced here.

What a notification is about.

```ts
export type NotificationKind = "stock_low" | "stock_negative" | "customer_overdue" | "supplier_overdue" | "supplier_due_soon" | "cash_difference" | "sync_refused" | "sync_stalled" | "service_grace" | "service_paused";
```

One current notification. Amounts are XAF, quantities thousandths, times ISO instants.

```ts
export interface ShopNotification {
    readonly key: string;
    readonly kind: NotificationKind;
    readonly severity: "warning" | "critical";
    readonly subjectId: string;
    readonly name: string;
    readonly facts: {
        readonly onHandThousandths?: number;
        readonly minimumThousandths?: number;
        readonly overdueMinor?: number;
        readonly dueSoonMinor?: number;
        readonly balanceMinor?: number;
        readonly oldestDueAt?: string;
        readonly nextDueAt?: string;
        readonly expectedMinor?: number;
        readonly countedMinor?: number;
        readonly differenceMinor?: number;
        readonly countedAt?: string;
        readonly error?: string;
        readonly lastSuccessAt?: string;
        readonly graceUntil?: string;
    };
    readonly fingerprint: string;
    readonly read: boolean;
    readonly announced: boolean;
}
```

Current notifications for the signed-in user at `now`, most serious first, bounded to `limit` (1–500, default 200)
with the unread count over all of them.

```ts
notifications(context: OperationContext, now: string, limit = 200): { readonly items: readonly ShopNotification[]; readonly unread: number; readonly truncated: boolean; };
```

Marks notifications read (and announced) for this user at `now`: the given keys, or every current one. Only current
notifications are marked, with their current fingerprint, so a later change raises them again. Marks for conditions
that have cleared are dropped. Returns the number marked.

```ts
markNotificationsRead(context: OperationContext, now: string, keys?: readonly string[]): number;
```

Marks notifications as announced (shown by the system) without reading them, so a desktop raises each changed
fact once. Returns the number newly marked.

```ts
markNotificationsAnnounced(context: OperationContext, now: string, keys: readonly string[]): number;
```

## [packages/core/src/application/operations.ts](../../packages/core/src/application/operations.ts)

Application inputs carry facts/IDs; authority and persistence time come from unlock ports.

```ts
export type CommandInput<T> = Omit<T, "businessId" | "userId" | "deviceId" | "recordedAt">;
```

Change a named customer leaves on their account instead of taking it back: cash handed over beyond the sale's
total, kept by the shop as the customer's advance. It is recorded as an ordinary customer payment into one of the
sale's cash accounts, so every till, old or new, applies it the same way.

```ts
export interface KeptChange {
    readonly id: string;
    readonly eventId: string;
    readonly accountId: string;
    readonly amountMinor: number;
}
```

Records a sale under local authority: sales.create, conditional discount/price
override/credit permissions and credit-limit enforcement. All related records
must belong to the installation's shop. Effects, v1 outbox and audit commit
together; any failure commits nothing. IDs/time of occurrence are caller facts.

With `keptChange`, the customer's change goes on their account in the same transaction, under customers.pay. The
sale must name a customer, be paid in full, and have been paid into that cash account. The payment's note is the
sale number and its time the sale's, which is how the receipt finds it ().

```ts
completeSale(context: OperationContext, input: CommandInput<Cart>, keptChange?: KeptChange): SaleTotals;
```

Receives stock under purchases.create, and purchases.pay when money is paid at the door.

```ts
recordPurchase(context: OperationContext, input: CommandInput<Delivery>): DeliveryTotals;
```

Receives customer payments under customers.pay; shop/account/customer and actor are checked atomically.

```ts
receiveCustomerPayment(context: OperationContext, input: CommandInput<CustomerPayment>): void;
```

Pays a supplier under purchases.pay; effects, event and audit roll back together.

```ts
paySupplier(context: OperationContext, input: CommandInput<SupplierPayment>): void;
```

Records a manual stock adjustment/count under the respective permission.
Sale/purchase/return movements must use their business workflow; they cannot
be forged through this command. Existing reference fields are refused here.

```ts
adjustStock(context: OperationContext, input: CommandInput<NewMovement>, eventId: string): void;
```

Records cash movement/transfer under cash.move; ADJUSTMENT also requires
cash.reconcile. Every leg is bound to the local actor/shop and checked for
account/branch scope before writing. Money and outbox remain atomic.

```ts
moveCash(context: OperationContext, input: {
    readonly movements: readonly CommandInput<CashMovement>[];
    readonly eventId: string;
}): void;
```

Authorized count comparison in XAF under cash.count; records audit, moves no money.

```ts
reconcileCash(context: OperationContext, accountId: string, countedMinor: number): Reconciliation;
```

Records an expense/reversal under expenses.create; reversal source must belong to this shop.

```ts
recordExpense(context: OperationContext, input: CommandInput<Expense>): void;
```

Saves a product under create/edit plus price/archive checks; refuses cross-shop upsert/category.

```ts
saveProduct(context: OperationContext, input: CommandInput<SaveProduct>): boolean;
```

Saves customer details under customers.manage; prevents global-ID upsert into another shop.

```ts
saveCustomer(context: OperationContext, input: CommandInput<SaveCustomer>): boolean;
```

Saves supplier details under suppliers.manage and checks existing identifier scope.

```ts
saveSupplier(context: OperationContext, input: CommandInput<SaveSupplier>): boolean;
```

Saves financial account details under finance.accounts.manage with shop-isolated identifiers.

```ts
saveAccount(context: OperationContext, input: CommandInput<SaveAccount>): boolean;
```

Counts actual stock in thousandths under inventory.count. Computes a compensating
delta against current stock (the counted branch's, when the shop has several)
inside the write transaction; a matching count records
audit only. Negative observed quantities and fractional indivisible units are refused.
An optional caller request ID becomes the movement ID and is kept in the local audit,
so adapters can recognise a retried count instead of recounting against later sales.

```ts
countStock(context: OperationContext, input: {
    readonly productId: string;
    readonly countedThousandths: number;
    readonly reason: string;
    readonly branchId?: string | undefined;
    readonly requestId?: string | undefined;
}): number;
```

Owner-authorized count plus explicit cash correction under cash.count/cash.reconcile/
cash.move. Recomputes the expected balance atomically and appends the adjustment and
audit. An equal count records audit only; a reason is always required. An optional
caller request ID names the adjustment and is kept in the local audit, so adapters can
recognise a retried correction instead of correcting again against later movements.

```ts
adjustCashToCount(context: OperationContext, input: {
    readonly accountId: string;
    readonly countedMinor: number;
    readonly reason: string;
    readonly branchId?: string | undefined;
    readonly requestId?: string | undefined;
}): Reconciliation;
```

Saves a category under product create/edit/archive authority. LWW details, v2 event and audit commit together.

```ts
saveCategory(context: OperationContext, input: Omit<Category, "businessId" | "updatedByDevice">): boolean;
```

Sets a product's additional categories under products.edit (last write wins, like product
details). The main category stays on the product; it is removed from this set, duplicates are
dropped, at most 20 remain, and every category must belong to the shop. The set, its v2 event and
audit commit together. Returns false when a newer set is already stored.

```ts
setProductCategories(context: OperationContext, input: {
    readonly productId: string;
    readonly categoryIds: readonly string[];
    readonly updatedAt: string;
}): boolean;
```

Saves the shop's tax settings under business.settings (ADR-033): prices include tax, and new sale lines carry the
standard rate (or a product's own rule) while tax is enabled. Last write wins by `updatedAt`, then device; returns
false when a newer version is stored. Settings, `TAX_SETTINGS_SET` and audit commit together.

```ts
setTaxSettings(context: OperationContext, input: Omit<TaxSettings, "updatedAt"> & {
    readonly updatedAt: string;
}): boolean;
```

Sets a product's tax rule under products.edit (ADR-033): the standard rate, exempt, or its own rate (1–10000 basis
points). Last write wins per product; false when a newer rule is stored. Rule, `PRODUCT_TAX_SET` and audit commit
together. Past sales keep the rate they were sold at.

```ts
setProductTaxRule(context: OperationContext, input: {
    readonly productId: string;
} & ProductTaxRule & {
    readonly updatedAt: string;
}): boolean;
```

Sets payment terms in days (ADR-033): the shop default for customers or suppliers under business.settings, or one
customer's (customers.manage) or supplier's (suppliers.manage) own terms; `days: null` returns a party to the shop
default. Last write wins per key; false when a newer record is stored. Terms, `PAYMENT_TERMS_SET` and audit commit
together. Due dates are derived, so this re-ages existing debts without editing any ledger.

```ts
setPaymentTerms(context: OperationContext, input: {
    readonly party: TermsParty;
    readonly partyId?: string | undefined;
    readonly days: number | null;
    readonly updatedAt: string;
}): boolean;
```

## [packages/core/src/application/pairing.ts](../../packages/core/src/application/pairing.ts)

128-bit comparison fingerprint. Compare directly with the owner through an independent channel.

```ts
pairingFingerprint(publicKey: string, crypto: TransferCrypto): Promise<string>;
```

Both installations display this exact request-bound comparison code before owner approval.

```ts
pairingVerificationCode(request: SignedPairingRequest, crypto: TransferCrypto): Promise<string>;
```

Issues an identity-only, expiring invitation. It grants no authority until owner confirmation.

```ts
createPairingInvite(context: OperationContext, crypto: TransferCrypto, authorityAllowed = false): Promise<TransferDocument>;
```

Authenticated invitation summary for independent fingerprint comparison; does not initialize a shop.

```ts
previewPairingInvite(file: Uint8Array, crypto: TransferCrypto): Promise<{ businessName: string; ownerName: string; fingerprint: string; authorityAllowed: boolean; }>;
```

Reserves fresh IDs durably before native provisioning. Repeating an interrupted acceptance reuses those IDs.

```ts
acceptPairingInvite(file: Uint8Array, input: {
    confirmedFingerprint: string;
    label: string;
    platform: "ANDROID" | "IOS" | "DESKTOP";
    secret: string;
    existingContext?: OperationContext;
}, ports: WorkspacePorts, crypto: TransferCrypto): Promise<{ document: TransferDocument; verificationCode: string; }>;
```

Verifies the native-key possession request for independent code comparison; has no trust effects.

```ts
previewPairingRequest(file: Uint8Array, crypto: TransferCrypto): Promise<{ deviceLabel: string; businessName: string; verificationCode: string; authorityAllowed: boolean; }>;
```

Owner confirmation pins the exact fresh key and emits an encrypted recipient-bound sanitized baseline.

```ts
confirmPairing(context: OperationContext, file: Uint8Array, confirmedCode: string, password: string, files: SnapshotPort, crypto: TransferCrypto): Promise<TransferDocument>;
```

Finishes only the exact approved enrollment. Existing recovery re-pairing adds trust while preserving newer local history.

```ts
completePairing(file: Uint8Array, password: string, confirmedCode: string, ports: WorkspacePorts, files: SnapshotPort, crypto: TransferCrypto, existingContext?: OperationContext): Promise<{ database: Database; installation: Installation; }>;
```

Removes only pending public enrollment state. No initialized business/history is deleted.

```ts
cancelPairing(ports: WorkspacePorts): void;
```

Cancels an unused source invitation. A confirmed pin must be revoked explicitly.

```ts
cancelPairingInvite(context: OperationContext, inviteId: string): void;
```

## [packages/core/src/application/photos.ts](../../packages/core/src/application/photos.ts)

Stores or replaces a product's photo under products.edit. The bytes are checked
(WebP or JPEG, size and dimension limits) and hashed before the write; the shop's
total is checked inside it against . Photos are device-local until photo transfer exists:
no exchange event is journaled, only a local audit entry with the digest.

```ts
setProductPhoto(context: OperationContext, input: {
    readonly productId: string;
    readonly bytes: Uint8Array;
}, crypto: Pick<TransferCrypto, "sha256Bytes">): Promise<{ digest: string; }>;
```

Removes a product's photo under products.edit; returns whether there was one.

```ts
removeProductPhoto(context: OperationContext, productId: string): boolean;
```

A product's photo under products.view, or undefined when this device holds none.

```ts
productPhoto(context: OperationContext, productId: string): ProductPhoto;
```

A product's photo digest under products.view, without the bytes.

```ts
productPhotoDigest(context: OperationContext, productId: string): string;
```

Photo digests for up to 100 products in one read, under products.view; products without a photo
on this device are absent. Lists use the digest as a cache key and load bytes for visible rows only.

```ts
productPhotoDigests(context: OperationContext, productIds: readonly string[]): ReadonlyMap<string, string>;
```

How many photos the shop holds against its storage limit, under products.view.

```ts
photoUsage(context: OperationContext): { photos: number; bytes: number; limitBytes: number; };
```

## [packages/core/src/application/platform-check.ts](../../packages/core/src/application/platform-check.ts)

One named check: whether the runtime's adapter gave the known answer, how long it took, and why not.

```ts
export interface PlatformCheckResult {
    readonly name: string;
    readonly ok: boolean;
    readonly ms: number;
    readonly detail?: string;
}
```

Every check of one runtime; `ok` only when all passed.

```ts
export interface PlatformCheck {
    readonly ok: boolean;
    readonly checks: readonly PlatformCheckResult[];
}
```

Runs the platform adapter of the runtime that ships (Node for CLI and server, Electron's BoringSSL for desktop, Hermes
and quick-crypto for mobile) against known answers, then opens the golden ADR-035 container with its password and its
recovery key and round-trips a new one. Node tests on another runtime cannot prove these primitives exist where the
app runs; this does, on the device or in the packaged app. Pass `sealing` to cover end-to-end sync primitives too.
Never throws; inputs are public test vectors, so failure details hold no secrets.

```ts
runPlatformCheck(crypto: TransferCrypto, sealing?: SealingCrypto): Promise<PlatformCheck>;
```

## [packages/core/src/application/queries.ts](../../packages/core/src/application/queries.ts)

Stable keyset continuation for catalogue queries; reset after a domain invalidation.

```ts
export interface Cursor {
    readonly name: string;
    readonly id: string;
}
```

Bounded page of local records. A missing cursor means the end of the result.

```ts
export interface Page<T> {
    readonly items: readonly T[];
    readonly next?: Cursor | undefined;
}
```

Literal prefix search by name or phone/code; limit 1–100, default 30.

```ts
export interface PageQuery {
    readonly term?: string;
    readonly after?: Cursor;
    readonly limit?: number;
    readonly includeInactive?: boolean;
    readonly categoryId?: string | undefined;
}
```

Reads a bounded product catalogue under current products.view authority.

```ts
products(context: OperationContext, query: PageQuery = {}): Page<Product>;
```

Exact barcode lookup for keyboard scanners. An ambiguous/absent code is never guessed.

```ts
scannedProduct(context: OperationContext, barcode: string): Product;
```

Reads customer details and balances under customers.view, including archived rows on request.

```ts
customers(context: OperationContext, query: PageQuery = {}): Page<Customer & { outstandingMinor: number; }>;
```

Reads supplier details and payable balances under suppliers.view.

```ts
suppliers(context: OperationContext, query: PageQuery = {}): Page<Supplier & { outstandingMinor: number; }>;
```

Payment account names only, with no balances; available to currently authorized payment/checkout actors.

```ts
paymentAccounts(context: OperationContext, query: PageQuery = {}): Page<Account>;
```

Finance account balances require cash.view as well as finance.accounts.view.

```ts
accounts(context: OperationContext, query: PageQuery = {}): Page<Account & { onHandMinor: number; }>;
```

Reads actual signed stock under inventory.view; refuses a foreign/missing product.

```ts
productStock(context: OperationContext, productId: string): StockLevel;
```

Signed stock for up to 100 products of this shop in one read, under inventory.view. Products of
another shop or unknown IDs are absent from the result rather than refused, so a visible list page
can ask for its rows without a query per row.

```ts
stockLevels(context: OperationContext, productIds: readonly string[]): ReadonlyMap<string, number>;
```

Immutable receipt facts with historical line names/prices and exact totals.

```ts
export interface Receipt {
    readonly id: string;
    readonly number: string;
    readonly occurredAt: string;
    readonly customerName?: string | undefined;
    readonly lines: readonly {
        id: string;
        productId: string;
        name: string;
        quantity: number;
        unitPriceMinor: number;
        discountMinor: number;
    }[];
    readonly payments: readonly {
        id: string;
        accountId: string;
        name: string;
        amountMinor: number;
    }[];
    readonly returnedMinor: number;
    readonly discountMinor: number;
    readonly totalMinor: number;
    readonly paidMinor: number;
    readonly outstandingMinor: number;
    readonly customerAccount?: CustomerSettlement | undefined;
    readonly tax?: {
        readonly label: string;
        readonly registration?: string | undefined;
        readonly groups: readonly TaxGroup[];
    } | undefined;
    readonly changeKeptMinor?: number | undefined;
}
```

One row of the receipt history: enough to recognize a sale without opening it.

```ts
export interface ReceiptSummary {
    readonly id: string;
    readonly number: string;
    readonly occurredAt: string;
    readonly totalMinor: number;
    readonly paidMinor: number;
    readonly outstandingMinor: number;
    readonly customerName?: string;
}
```

Reads one persisted receipt. sales.view_own enforces the creating actor inside the query.

```ts
receipt(context: OperationContext, saleId: string): Receipt;
```

Keyset receipt history, newest first, bounded 1–100 and scoped to current sales authority.

```ts
receipts(context: OperationContext, query: PageQuery = {}): Page<ReceiptSummary>;
```

Bounded customer/supplier ledger page, newest first; includes running `balance_minor` and customer sale `sale_total_minor`/`sale_paid_minor` facts.

```ts
statement(context: OperationContext, kind: "customer" | "supplier", id: string, query: PageQuery = {}): Page<Row>;
```

Current effective UI capabilities, re-read from local membership; commands still authorize independently.

```ts

```

Bounded manual inventory history under inventory.view, including negative movements.


Bounded purchase history under purchases.view, with exact totals from historical costs.

```ts

```

Bounded categories under products.view. Inactive categories can remain attached to historic products.

```ts
categories(context: OperationContext, query: PageQuery = {}): Page<Category>;
```

Current receivable/payable summary under the domain view permission, refreshed independently of a selected row.

```ts
personBalance(context: OperationContext, kind: "customer" | "supplier", id: string): number;
```

One category within current products.view scope, including inactive retained associations.

```ts
category(context: OperationContext, id: string): Category;
```

## [packages/core/src/application/report-views.ts](../../packages/core/src/application/report-views.ts)

Report kinds, each read under its own core report permission.

```ts
export type ReportKind = "dashboard" | "sales" | "salesByProduct" | "salesByCategory" | "salesByAccount" | "salesByUser" | "cashFlow" | "valuation" | "fastMoving" | "stockAlerts" | "customerDebt" | "supplierDebt" | "purchases" | "tax";
```

Every report kind, in the order the clients list them.

```ts
const REPORT_KINDS: readonly ReportKind[];
```

Reports of the present state (stock and balances owed). They ignore `from`/`until`, which are not validated for them.

```ts
const UNDATED_REPORT_KINDS: ReadonlySet<ReportKind>;
```

The permission each report kind is read under.

```ts
const REPORT_PERMISSION: Readonly<Record<ReportKind, "reports.financial" | "reports.sales" | "reports.inventory" | "reports.customers" | "reports.suppliers">>;
```

A labelled total; `count` is lines, transactions or entries depending on the report.

```ts
export interface ReportLine {
    readonly key: string;
    readonly label: string;
    readonly totalMinor: number;
    readonly count: number;
}
```

Serializable report results. Row lists are capped; `truncated` says more rows exist. Totals stay exact.

```ts
export type ReportResult = {
    readonly kind: "dashboard";
    readonly salesMinor: number;
    readonly transactions: number;
    readonly grossProfitMinor: number;
    readonly costMinor: number;
    readonly linesWithoutCost: number;
    readonly expensesMinor: number;
    readonly netResultMinor: number;
    readonly customerDebtMinor: number;
    readonly supplierDebtMinor: number;
    readonly lowStock: number;
} | {
    readonly kind: "sales";
    readonly totalMinor: number;
    readonly transactions: number;
    readonly itemsSoldThousandths: number;
} | {
    readonly kind: "salesByProduct" | "salesByCategory" | "salesByAccount" | "salesByUser" | "customerDebt" | "supplierDebt";
    readonly lines: readonly ReportLine[];
    readonly truncated: boolean;
} | {
    readonly kind: "cashFlow";
    readonly accounts: readonly {
        readonly accountId: string;
        readonly name: string;
        readonly inMinor: number;
        readonly outMinor: number;
        readonly netMinor: number;
        readonly closingMinor: number;
    }[];
} | {
    readonly kind: "valuation";
    readonly totalMinor: number;
    readonly uncosted: number;
    readonly truncated: boolean;
    readonly products: readonly {
        readonly productId: string;
        readonly name: string;
        readonly onHandThousandths: number;
        readonly unitCostMinor?: number;
        readonly valueMinor?: number;
    }[];
} | {
    readonly kind: "fastMoving";
    readonly products: readonly {
        readonly productId: string;
        readonly name: string;
        readonly soldThousandths: number;
        readonly revenueMinor: number;
        readonly onHandThousandths: number;
    }[];
} | {
    readonly kind: "stockAlerts";
    readonly products: readonly {
        readonly productId: string;
        readonly name: string;
        readonly onHandThousandths: number;
    }[];
    readonly truncated: boolean;
} | {
    readonly kind: "purchases";
    readonly totalMinor: number;
    readonly deliveries: number;
    readonly bySupplier: readonly ReportLine[];
    readonly truncated: boolean;
} | {
    readonly kind: "tax";
    readonly label: string;
    readonly grossMinor: number;
    readonly taxMinor: number;
    readonly netMinor: number;
    readonly groups: readonly {
        readonly rateBasisPoints: number;
        readonly grossMinor: number;
        readonly taxMinor: number;
        readonly netMinor: number;
    }[];
};
```

How an analytics trend is sliced; weeks start on Monday.

```ts
export type AnalyticsBucket = "hour" | "day" | "week" | "month";
```

One trend point keyed by local `YYYY-MM-DDTHH`, `YYYY-MM-DD` (day or week's Monday) or `YYYY-MM`; profit only with reports.financial.

```ts
export interface AnalyticsPoint {
    readonly key: string;
    readonly revenueMinor: number;
    readonly transactions: number;
    readonly grossProfitMinor?: number;
}
```

Sales started in one local weekday (0 = Sunday) and hour, summed over the period; empty cells are absent.

```ts
export interface AnalyticsCell {
    readonly weekday: number;
    readonly hour: number;
    readonly transactions: number;
    readonly revenueMinor: number;
}
```

A product's net sales over the period, before bill discounts; profit only with reports.financial.

```ts
export interface AnalyticsProduct {
    readonly productId: string;
    readonly name: string;
    readonly soldThousandths: number;
    readonly revenueMinor: number;
    readonly grossProfitMinor?: number;
}
```

A named customer's net buying over the period.

```ts
export interface AnalyticsCustomer {
    readonly customerId: string;
    readonly name: string;
    readonly transactions: number;
    readonly revenueMinor: number;
    readonly lastPurchaseAt: string;
}
```

Light analytics for one period. Sections the actor may not read are absent rather than failing the
whole view: `profit` and per-point/product profit need reports.financial, `customers` needs
reports.customers; the rest needs reports.sales. `previous` is the equal-length period just before.

```ts
export interface AnalyticsResult {
    readonly bucket: AnalyticsBucket;
    readonly sales: {
        readonly revenueMinor: number;
        readonly transactions: number;
        readonly itemsSoldThousandths: number;
        readonly averageBasketMinor: number;
    };
    readonly previous: {
        readonly revenueMinor: number;
        readonly transactions: number;
        readonly grossProfitMinor?: number;
    };
    readonly profit?: {
        readonly grossProfitMinor: number;
        readonly costMinor: number;
        readonly marginBasisPoints?: number;
        readonly linesWithoutCost: number;
        readonly expensesMinor: number;
        readonly netResultMinor: number;
    };
    readonly trend: readonly AnalyticsPoint[];
    readonly activity: readonly AnalyticsCell[];
    readonly products: readonly AnalyticsProduct[];
    readonly productsTruncated: boolean;
    readonly categories: readonly ReportLine[];
    readonly accounts: readonly ReportLine[];
    readonly customers?: readonly AnalyticsCustomer[];
}
```

Row cap for client views. Desktop shows up to 500 rows; phones pass a smaller cap.

```ts
export interface ViewOptions {
    readonly rows?: number;
}
```

One report under its core permission over `[from, until)` (ISO instants from shared local-day
rules; empty or decade-plus periods are refused).  ignore the period. Row lists are bounded by `options.rows`
(default and maximum 500, stock alerts 100) with a `truncated` flag; totals stay exact.

```ts
reportView(context: OperationContext, kind: ReportKind, from: string, until: string, options: ViewOptions = {}): ReportResult;
```

Light analytics for one period under reports.sales, with profit and customer sections only for
actors holding reports.financial and reports.customers. The period is at most about ten years; the
offset is the shop's local minutes east of UTC (at most ±840); an explicit bucket must keep the
trend to 1,000 points. Product and share lists are bounded by `options.rows`.

```ts
analyticsView(context: OperationContext, input: {
    readonly from: string;
    readonly until: string;
    readonly offsetMinutes: number;
    readonly bucket?: AnalyticsBucket | undefined;
}, options: ViewOptions = {}): AnalyticsResult;
```
